### [1] [StateAct: Program State, before Pixels, for Long-Horizon Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22798)
*Yan Yang,Xiangru Jian,Ziyang Luo,Zirui Zhao,Yutong Dai,Ziji Shi,Hanshu Yan,Jun Hao Liew,Silvio Savarese,Junnan Li*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: StateAct通过代码优先的多智能体框架直接操作程序状态，仅少量使用GUI，显著提升了性能并降低了成本，将瓶颈从感知转向推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 屏幕截图是有损的，不同程序状态可能产生相同像素，而代码可直接访问状态，因此开发代码优先的智能体以减少对感知的依赖。

Method: 构建StateAct框架：主智能体通过代码直接操作程序状态，仅在必要时由GUI子智能体处理屏幕点击；独立的完成门控验证输出；通过子智能体分发子目标保持上下文聚焦。

Result: 在OSWorld 2.0上，二元成功率从20.6%升至26.9%，部分成功率从54.8%升至61.6%，成本降低约9倍；代码-only变体部分成功率45.9%低于基线。

Conclusion: 将动作、验证和记忆基于状态（状态基础化）将主要瓶颈从感知转移到推理，失败更多源于智能体的思考而非视觉输入。

中文摘要：计算机使用代理通常通过加强感知来改进：更好的模型用于读取屏幕截图并选择点击位置。然而，屏幕截图只是底层程序状态的有损渲染，例如，保存任务数据的文件、应用程序后端和DOM。不同的状态可以产生相同的像素，而代码可以直接检查和修改该状态。StateAct是一个围绕这一区别构建的代码优先、多智能体框架。它的主要智能体通过使用代码直接处理程序状态，而专门的GUI子智能体在少数需要它的子目标上处理屏幕截图和点击交互，在108个任务中只有28个任务需要，且仅占主要智能体步骤的1.1%。对程序状态的直接访问也支持验证：一个独立的完成门控双重检查保存的结果是否存在结构故障，例如，输出缺失、未保存或写入错误路径。为了在数百个步骤中保持正轨，主要智能体将子目标交给新的子智能体，保持其自身上下文的聚焦。在OSWorld 2.0上，StateAct将Claude Opus 4.8的二元成功率从20.6%提升到26.9%，部分成功率从54.8%提升到61.6%，每任务成本比同一模型仅使用屏幕截图驱动低约9倍；没有GUI子智能体的纯代码变体仅达到45.9%的部分成功率，低于基于屏幕截图的基线54.8%。总的来说，将动作、验证和记忆基于状态（我们称之为状态基础化）将主要瓶颈从感知转移到了推理：失败更多地取决于智能体的思考而非其看到的内容。

Original abstract: Computer-use agents are usually improved by strengthening perception: better models for reading a screenshot and choosing where to click. Yet a screenshot is only a lossy rendering of the underlying program state, e.g., the files, application backends, and DOM that hold the task data. Different states can produce the same pixels, while code can inspect and modify that state directly. StateAct is a code-first, multi-agent harness built around this distinction. Its main agent works directly with program state by using code, while a dedicated GUI subagent handles screenshot-and-click interaction on the few subgoals that need it, just 28 of 108 tasks and 1.1% of main-agent steps. The same direct access to program state also supports verification: an independent finish gate double-checks the saved result for structural failures, e.g., output that is missing, unsaved, or written to the wrong path. To stay on track over hundreds of steps, the main agent hands subgoals to fresh subagents, keeping its own context focused. On OSWorld 2.0, StateAct lifts Claude Opus 4.8 from 20.6% to 26.9% on binary success, and from 54.8% to 61.6% on partial success, at ~ 9x lower cost per task than the same model driven by screenshots alone; a code-only variant with no GUI subagent reaches only 45.9% partial, below that screenshot-based baseline's 54.8%. In general, grounding action, verification, and memory in state, what we call state-grounding, shifts the main bottleneck from perception toward reasoning: failures depend more on what the agent thinks than on what it sees.

</details>


### [2] [The Best Programming Language for Tokenmaxxing: An Investigation of Coding Agent Behavior Across Programming Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22807)
*Zixuan Wu,Carolyn Jane Anderson,Arjun Guha*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 本文首次揭示编程语言对编码代理的token成本有显著影响，并分析了原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码代理在多语言场景下有效，但未考虑不同语言间的token成本差异。

Method: 在Python、Java、Rust和OCaml上评估五种模型；通过重新执行中间解决方案和轨迹文本分析来研究token消耗差异的原因。

Result: 语言间token消耗存在显著且跨模型一致的差异，原因包括代理在陌生语言中反复产生非编译代码、修改已有通过的解决方案、在注释中规划、不信任测试以及用Python原型化。

Conclusion: 按语言的token效率是重要的基准指标，对多语言代理开发和成本优化有指导意义。

中文摘要：尽管编码代理现在在各种编程语言中都非常有效，但本文首次表明，成本（以token计）可能因编程语言而有显著差异。我们评估了五种最近的模型在Python、Java、Rust和OCaml编程问题上的表现。我们仔细控制了问题难度，并表明可能存在跨模型一致的token消耗的显著变化。为了理解原因，我们分析了代理轨迹的结构和内容。首先，我们重新执行每个中间解决方案，并将每个轨迹抽象为测试结果向量的序列，然后标记连续解决方案之间的工作。这揭示了代理在陌生语言中反复产生无法编译的解决方案，并修改已经通过的解决方案。其次，我们分析轨迹文本，发现代理在代码注释中规划解决方案，不信任提供的测试而倾向于他们自己发明的输入，并通过在Python中制作原型来绕过不熟悉的目标语言。我们的结果表明，按语言的token效率是在基准测试和开发多语言代理时应考虑的指标，并且对于token最大化者来说，是指导最昂贵语言的指南。

Original abstract: Although coding agents are now very effective in a variety of programming languages, this paper first shows that the cost (in tokens) can very significantly by programming language. We evaluate five recent models on programming problems in Python, Java, Rust, and OCaml. We carefully control for problem difficulty, and show that there can be stark variation in token consumption that is consistent across models. To understand why, we analyze both the structure and content of agent trajectories. First, we re-execute every intermediate solution and abstract each trajectory as a sequence of test-outcome vectors, then label the work between successive solutions. This reveals agents repeatedly producing noncompiling solutions in unfamiliar languages and revising solutions that already pass. Second, we analyze trajectory text, finding that agents plan solutions in code comments, distrust the provided tests in favor of inputs they invent, and sidestep unfamiliar target languages by prototyping in Python.
  Our results show that by-language token efficiency is a metric that should be considered when benchmarking and developing multilingual agents, and, for the tokenmaxxer, a guide to the most expensive language to work in.

</details>


### [3] [Do Coverage and Mutation Scores of LLM-Generated Test Suites Correlate with Their Effectiveness? (Replicability Study)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22880)
*Junda Zhao,Shurui Zhou,Eldan Cohen*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: 24 pages, 4 figures, 14 tables. Accepted at ISSTA 2026; to appear in Proceedings of the ACM on Software Engineering (PACMSE), Vol. 3, No. ISSTA, Article ISSTA002

DOI: [10.1145/3832093](https://doi.org/10.1145/3832093)

Author affiliations: Eldan Cohen: University of Toronto

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 通过大规模复制研究，发现覆盖率和变异指标在LLM生成测试中的有效性取决于上下文，且测试套件大小不是主要混杂因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证先前关于人类编写测试的结论（覆盖率和变异与真实缺陷检测相关性受测试套件大小影响）是否适用于LLM生成的测试。

Method: 使用多种LLM生成多样化的测试套件，进行大规模复制研究，重新检验覆盖率、变异和真实缺陷检测的关系。

Result: 覆盖率和变异的有用性取决于上下文：在无缺陷代码场景下有效，在有缺陷场景下不可靠；测试套件大小不是主要混杂因素。

Conclusion: 需要根据具体场景使用覆盖率和变异指标，为评估LLM生成的测试提供了指导。

中文摘要：最近大型语言模型（LLM）的进展推动了对使用LLM自动化测试生成的兴趣。以往的工作通常使用代码覆盖率和变异分数等代理指标来评估生成的测试套件。然而，Inozemtseva等人和Papadakis等人的研究表明，对于人类编写的测试，一旦控制测试套件大小，覆盖率、变异和真实缺陷检测之间的相关性可能基本消失，这引发了对基于代理指标评估有效性的质疑。目前尚不清楚这些结论是否适用于LLM生成的测试，因为主流的基于LLM的测试生成工作流程与传统方法有很大不同。在本文中，我们使用一组多样化的LLM生成的广泛测试套件，对这两项先前工作进行了大规模复制研究，重新审视了覆盖率、变异和真实缺陷检测有效性之间的关系。我们的发现与先前结果有很大差异。我们表明，覆盖率和变异的有用性高度依赖于上下文：在回归测试场景中，提供给LLM的代码可以合理地假设为无缺陷，这些指标可以在跨模型比较时提供有意义的信号；在另一种常见场景中，被测代码可能已有缺陷，目标是暴露代码中的缺陷，它们不再作为可靠的指标。我们还发现，对于LLM生成的测试，几乎没有证据表明测试套件大小是覆盖率、变异和真实缺陷检测之间相关性的主要混杂因素。基于这些发现，我们讨论了如何解释先前研究的结果，并为评估基于LLM的测试生成提供了可操作的指导。

Original abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have driven growing interest in using LLMs to automate test generation. Prior work commonly evaluates generated test suites using proxy metrics such as code coverage and mutation score. However, studies by Inozemtseva et al. and Papadakis et al. show that, for human-written tests, correlations among coverage, mutation, and real-bug detection can largely vanish once test suite size is controlled, raising concerns about the validity of evaluations based on proxy metrics. It also remains unclear whether these conclusions carry over to LLM-generated tests, given that prevailing LLM-based test-generation workflows differ substantially from traditional approaches.
  In this paper, we conduct a large-scale replication study of these two prior works using a wide range of test suites generated by a diverse set of LLMs, and re-examine the relationships among coverage, mutation, and real-bug detection effectiveness. Our findings diverge substantially from prior results. We show that the usefulness of coverage and mutation is highly context-dependent: in regression-style settings where the code provided to the LLM can be reasonably assumed bug-free, these metrics can provide meaningful signals when comparing across models; in another common scenario where the code-under-test may already be buggy and the goal is to expose the bug within the code-under-test, they no longer serve as reliable indicators. We also find little evidence that test suite size is a dominant confounder for correlations among coverage, mutation, and real-bug detection for LLM-generated tests. Based on these findings, we discuss how to interpret results from prior studies and provide actionable guidance for evaluating LLM-based test generation.

</details>


### [4] [Evaluating and Mitigating the Misguidance Effect of Buggy Code in LLM-Generated Unit Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22883)
*Junda Zhao,Shurui Zhou,Eldan Cohen*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: 24 pages, 7 figures, 12 tables. Accepted at ISSTA 2026; to appear in Proceedings of the ACM on Software Engineering (PACMSE), Vol. 3, No. ISSTA, Article ISSTA113

DOI: [10.1145/3832204](https://doi.org/10.1145/3832204)

Author affiliations: Eldan Cohen: University of Toronto

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 提出一种新指标量化LLM生成单元测试时错误代码的误导效应，并引入规范提示范式来减轻该效应，实验证明有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在含错误代码提示下生成的测试质量下降，需要定量评估误导效应并寻找缓解方法。

Method: 定义误导效应指标，分析LLM内部偏好，提出基于规范文档字符串的提示范式替代被测代码。

Result: 规范提示显著减少误导性测试，增加有效测试，改善多轮反馈流程，适用于错误和无错误代码。

Conclusion: 基于规范的提示是缓解LLM生成单元测试中错误代码误导的有效策略。

中文摘要：尽管大型语言模型（LLM）在自动化单元测试生成方面表现出巨大潜力，但近期研究表明，当模型被提示带有错误代码时，生成测试的质量可能会受到负面影响。本文提出了一种新的度量标准，用以定量测量“误导效应”——即错误代码引导LLM生成验证其错误行为而非揭露错误的测试。我们的分析揭示，用错误代码提示LLM会产生严重的双重影响：它显著增加了断言错误行为的“误导性测试”，同时抑制了有效的、发现错误的测试生成。我们进一步从模型内部视角证实了这种效应，显示错误代码使LLM的偏好偏向于断言相同错误行为的测试。为应对这一问题，我们引入并验证了一种基于规范的单元测试生成范式，该范式用LLM生成的规范文档字符串替换提示中的被测代码。我们的结果表明，该范式能有效减少误导性测试，同时显著增加有效测试，改进了多轮、反馈驱动的测试生成流程，并且对错误代码和无错误代码均适用。总体而言，这些结果表明，基于规范的提示是缓解LLM生成单元测试中错误代码误导的一种有前景的策略。

Original abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) show great promise for automating unit test generation, recent studies suggest that the quality of generated tests can be negatively impacted when models are prompted with buggy code. This paper presents a new metric to quantitatively measure the "misguidance effect," a phenomenon where buggy code steers LLMs toward generating tests that validate its erroneous behavior rather than expose it. Our analysis reveals that prompting LLMs with buggy code has a severe, twofold impact: it significantly increases "misguided tests" that assert incorrect behavior while simultaneously suppressing the generation of effective, bug-finding tests. We further corroborate this effect from a model-internal perspective, showing that buggy code skews LLMs' preference toward tests that assert the same erroneous behavior. To counter this, we introduce and validate a specification-based unit test generation paradigm that replaces the code under test in the prompt with an LLM-generated specification docstring. Our results show that this paradigm effectively reduces misguided tests while substantially increasing effective tests, improves multi-round, feedback-driven test generation pipelines, and remains applicable to both buggy and bug-free code. Overall, these results suggest that specification-based prompting is a promising strategy for mitigating misguidance from buggy code in LLM-generated unit tests.

</details>


### [5] [AssumptionMiner: Extracting, Tracing, and Revising Implicit Assumptions in LLM Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22898)
*Jie "JW" Wu*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: 20 pages, 6 figures, 9 tables. Submitted to IEEE Transactions on Software Engineering. Replication package: https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.21535058

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 提出了AssumptionMiner框架，通过显式提取隐式假设并支持针对性代码重新生成，提高了LLM代码生成的透明度和可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的代码可能因隐式假设与开发者意图不符而出现问题，而现有方法无法揭示这些假设。

Method: 提出AssumptionMiner框架，包括显式假设层（结构化表示约束和设计决策）、基于AST的依赖图实现针对性重新生成，并构建了包含180个模糊编程任务的基准。

Result: 置信度加权集成在假设提取上F1=0.816（比离线基线高3.6倍）；AST引导定位比基线更精确；针对性重新生成修改代码更少。

Conclusion: 显式化假设可提升LLM代码生成的可控性和透明度。

中文摘要：大语言模型（LLM）从自然语言提示生成代码，然而现实世界的提示很少提供完整的规范。当提示未指定输入格式、错误处理或设计决策时，LLM会用隐含的假设来填补这些空白，这些假设塑造了生成代码的行为和正确性。由于这些假设仍然是隐藏的，生成的代码可能通过测试但违反开发者意图。我们提出了AssumptionMiner，一个将隐式假设作为LLM代码生成的一等工件的框架。除了代码外，AssumptionMiner还生成一个显式的假设层，一个推断出的约束和设计决策的结构化表示，开发者可以检查、确认或修改。基于AST的依赖图使得仅重新生成受修改假设影响的代码成为可能。我们还引入了一个包含180个模糊编程任务和676个标注假设的基准，包括一个用于评估代码定位的人工验证子集。我们评估了假设提取、代码定位和假设引导的重新生成。在开源LLM中，一个置信度加权的集成在假设提取上达到了0.816的F1分数，比最强的离线基线提高了3.6倍。在人工验证的定位基准上，AST引导的定位比基于关键词和整个文件的基线识别出更精确的代码区域。在假设修订期间，有针对性的重新生成比非选择性替代方案修改更少的代码，同时暴露了处理级联编辑的挑战。这些结果表明，使假设显式化提高了基于LLM的代码生成的透明度和可控性。

Original abstract: Large language models (LLMs) generate code from natural-language prompts, yet real-world prompts rarely provide complete specifications. When prompts leave input formats, error handling, or design decisions unspecified, LLMs fill these gaps with implicit assumptions that shape the generated code's behavior and correctness. Because these assumptions remain hidden, generated code may satisfy tests while violating developer intent. We present AssumptionMiner, a framework that makes implicit assumptions a first-class artifact of LLM-based code generation. In addition to code, AssumptionMiner produces an explicit assumption layer, a structured representation of inferred constraints and design decisions that developers can inspect, confirm, or revise. An AST-based dependency graph enables targeted regeneration of only the code affected by a revised assumption. We also introduce a benchmark of 180 ambiguous programming tasks with 676 annotated assumptions, including a human-verified subset for evaluating code localization. We evaluate assumption extraction, code localization, and assumption-guided regeneration. Across open-source LLMs, a confidence-weighted ensemble achieves an F1 score of 0.816 for assumption extraction, improving on the strongest offline baseline by 3.6x. On the human-verified localization benchmark, AST-guided localization identifies more precise code regions than keyword-based and whole-file baselines. During assumption revision, targeted regeneration modifies less code than non-targeted alternatives while exposing challenges in handling cascading edits. These results demonstrate that making assumptions explicit improves the transparency and controllability of LLM-based code generation.

</details>


### [6] [Metamorphic Testing for Clinical ML Models: A Framework Proposal and Pilot Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22984)
*Jie JW Wu,Feiyu E,Bo Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: 4 pages, 1 figure, 4 tables. Accepted at the AIware 2026 arXiv Track

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 本文提出用蜕变测试评估临床预测模型的行为正确性，发现AUROC高的模型在临床合理性上存在大量违规，MT可作为补充指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AUROC仅衡量排序性能，可能掩盖违反医学常识的行为，如SOFA恶化时死亡风险预测降低，需要一种不依赖标签的行为正确性评估方法。

Method: 设计12个基于临床指南的蜕变关系，采用五层验证策略确保临床合理性，并在UCI心脏病数据集上进行可行性实验。

Result: 三个模型AUROC=0.849-0.900，但在5个MR上违规率27%-87%；注入符号错误后AUROC未变但MT违规率增加31-67个百分点。

Conclusion: 蜕变测试可作为传统性能指标的有效补充，用于检测临床预测模型的行为错误。

中文摘要：用于临床预测任务（如院内死亡率和脓毒症发作）的机器学习模型通常能达到很高的AUROC分数。然而，AUROC衡量的是排序性能而非临床合理性。一个模型可能在整体上对患者进行正确排序，但当患者的SOFA评分恶化时，却预测出较低的死亡风险，这与已有的医学知识相矛盾。本文提出将蜕变测试（MT）应用于临床机器学习模型，以评估行为正确性，而无需个体预测的真实标签。我们利用MIMIC-III和MIMIC-IV数据集，为三个ICU预测任务设计了12个候选蜕变关系（MR）的目录，每个MR都基于权威的临床指南。我们还提出了一种五层验证策略，以确保在部署前MR在临床上是合理的。作为可行性研究，我们在UCI心脏病数据集上评估该方法。尽管三个临床模型实现了强大的预测性能（AUROC = 0.849-0.900），但它们在五个试点MR上表现出27%至87%的MT违规率。一项注入故障实验进一步表明，血压特征中的符号取反错误未被AUROC检测到，但使MT违规率增加了31-67个百分点。这些发现表明，蜕变测试为评估临床预测模型的行为正确性提供了对传统性能指标的有价值的补充。

Original abstract: Machine learning models for clinical prediction tasks, such as in-hospital mortality and sepsis onset, routinely achieve high AUROC scores. However, AUROC measures ranking performance rather than clinical sensibility. A model may rank patients correctly overall while predicting a lower mortality risk when a patient's SOFA score worsens, contradicting established medical knowledge.
  This paper proposes applying metamorphic testing (MT) to clinical machine learning models to evaluate behavioral correctness without requiring ground-truth labels for individual predictions. We design a catalog of 12 candidate metamorphic relations (MRs) for three ICU prediction tasks using the MIMIC-III and MIMIC-IV datasets, with each MR grounded in an authoritative clinical guideline. We further propose a five-layer validation strategy to ensure that MRs are clinically sound before deployment.
  As a feasibility study, we evaluate the approach on the UCI Heart Disease dataset. Although the three clinical models achieve strong predictive performance (AUROC = 0.849-0.900), they exhibit MT violation rates ranging from 27% to 87% across five pilot MRs. An injected-fault experiment further shows that a sign-negation error in a blood pressure feature remains undetected by AUROC but increases the MT violation rate by 31-67 percentage points. These findings suggest that metamorphic testing provides a valuable complement to conventional performance metrics for assessing the behavioral correctness of clinical prediction models.

</details>


### [7] [Adversarial Test-Hardening for AI-Written Code: An Instrument Autopsy and a Pre-Registered Causal Estimate of the Critic Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23002)
*Jeff Otterson*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: 26 pages. Two pre-registered experiments; protocols, all run receipts, and analysis code at https://github.com/Jott2121/crucible

Author affiliations: Jeff Otterson: W. P. Carey School of Business, Arizona State University

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 本文提出一种对抗性测试强化循环，通过机械性预言和变异测试提高测试质量，并发现早期结果中的仪器伪影，展示了跨模型比较中的框架不对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码测试覆盖率仅记录运行内容而非验证内容，且模型间评估存在偏差，需要机械性验证方法。

Method: 采用对抗性测试强化循环，包括Tester和Critic模型，结合变异测试，所有判定机械性执行；并通过两个实验验证，使用冻结共享初始套件等设计控制混淆。

Result: 实验1杀死105个一次性生成遗漏的变异体；实验2中同谱系Critic杀死78%存活变异体，跨提供者配置显示正面差异但受单个重复主导。

Conclusion: 跨模型比较受框架不对称性影响，发布协议和代码以确保可重复性。

中文摘要：大型语言模型越来越多地编写代码以及用于检查代码的测试；覆盖率记录了运行了什么，而不是验证了什么。我们研究了一种在机械性预言下的对抗性测试强化循环：一个测试者模型编写测试，变异测试标记存活的注入缺陷，然后一个批评者模型编写测试以恰好杀死这些缺陷，每个判定均由机械性决定，因此没有模型评判另一个模型的输出。在实验1中，针对五个Python主题（其中一个同谱系循环的细胞无法评分），该循环杀死了105个一次性生成遗漏的变异体，且未丢失任何变异体，而跨谱系批评者问题返回了一个预先声明的空值。核心发现是一次尸检：一份早期分析报告称跨谱系效应p=9.5e-66，但这是一种仪器伪影——输出上限悄然截断了冗长模型，只有在对抗性审查已完成分析时才被发现。审查随后发现了另一个混淆因素：每个臂重新采样其自己的初始套件；实验2消除了这一点。在预先注册的冻结共享第0轮设计下（每个主题五个重复，种子预先提交），同谱系批评者轮次杀死了冻结初始套件留下的存活变异体的78%（平均增量杀死率0.783，95%聚类自助法区间[0.592, 0.935]），这是一个重复内的因果估计；跨提供者配置显示出正面的初步差异（比率差距0.178，95%区间[0.039, 0.347]；幅度受单个重复主导），且臂成本降低5.5倍。这比较了两个命名的模型-提供者-框架配置，而非孤立的谱系效应：部分差距来自一个配置已记录的运行故障，包括截断复发，现在被检测并评分，而非被掩盖。跨模型比较可能继承运行它们的框架的不对称性。我们发布了两个协议、所有收据以及分析代码。

Original abstract: Large language models increasingly write both code and the tests meant to check it; coverage records what ran, not what was verified. We study an adversarial test-hardening loop under a mechanical oracle: a Tester model writes tests, mutation testing names surviving injected defects, and a Critic model writes tests to kill exactly those, with every verdict decided mechanically, so no model judges another's output. In Experiment 1, on five Python subjects (one same-lineage-loop cell could not be scored), the loop killed 105 mutants that one-shot generation missed and lost none, and the cross-lineage-Critic question returned a pre-declared null. The central finding was an autopsy: an earlier analysis reported a cross-lineage effect at p = 9.5e-66 that was an instrument artifact, an output cap silently truncating the verbose model, caught only by adversarial review of the completed analysis. Review then found a further confound, each arm resampling its own initial suite; Experiment 2 removes it. Under a pre-registered frozen-shared-round-0 design (five replicates on each of four subjects, seeds committed in advance), same-lineage Critic rounds killed 78% of the survivors the frozen initial suite left standing (mean incremental kill rate 0.783, 95% cluster-bootstrap interval [0.592, 0.935]), a within-replicate causal estimate; the cross-provider configuration showed a positive pilot difference (rate gap 0.178, 95% interval [0.039, 0.347]; magnitude dominated by a single replicate) at 5.5x lower arm cost. This compares two named model-provider-harness configurations, not an isolated lineage effect: part of the gap is one configuration's receipted operational failures, including truncation recurrences, now detected and scored rather than laundered. Cross-model comparisons can inherit the asymmetries of the harness that runs them. We release both protocols, all receipts, and the analysis code.

</details>


### [8] [Bifrost: Empowering Pretrained Language Model with Fallibility Representation for Log-Based Fault Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23169)
*Minghua He,Tong Jia,Lingzhe Zhang,Chiming Duan,Xinlong Zhao,Leyi Pan,Cheng Wang,Kangjin Wang,Yinghao Yu,Liping Zhang,Yifan Wu,Ying Li*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: Accepted by ASE 2026 (Research Track)

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: Bifrost是一种基于自监督对比学习的日志表示学习方法，通过学习日志的易错表示，在异常检测、根因定位和故障识别任务上显著优于现有预训练语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法使用自然语言预训练模型表征日志，但无法充分捕获系统日志的多级故障信息，导致诊断效果不佳。

Method: 提出Bifrost，借鉴站点可靠性工程师经验，设计基于自监督对比学习的策略来学习日志的易错表示。

Result: 在三个公共系统和一个工业系统上，Bifrost在异常检测F1、根因定位HR@k和故障识别Macro-F1上分别平均提升9.83%、18.28%和20.88%。

Conclusion: Bifrost通过关注日志的易错表示，有效提升了故障诊断性能。

中文摘要：基于日志的故障诊断对于运行时调试和维护至关重要。现有的故障诊断方法使用在自然语言上预训练的语言模型（PLM）来表征日志。然而，系统故障体现在系统日志的多级结构中。在自然语言上预训练的PLM难以全面捕获多级故障信息，无法满足故障诊断的要求。我们将这种信息称为易错表示。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了一种新颖的日志表示学习方法Bifrost。它借鉴了站点可靠性工程师的日志分析经验，并精心设计了基于自监督对比学习的策略来学习日志的易错表示。在三个公共系统和一个工业ML-as-a-Service系统上，Bifrost生成的日志表示在异常检测的F1指标上平均超出现有PLM 9.83%，在根因定位的HR@k上平均超出18.28%，在故障识别的Macro-F1上平均超出20.88%。

Original abstract: Log-based fault diagnosis is crucial for runtime debugging and maintenance. Existing fault diagnosis methods use language models pre-trained on natural language (PLMs) for log representation. However, system faults are reflected in the multi-level structure of system logs. PLMs pre-trained on natural language struggle to comprehensively capture multi-level fault information, failing to meet the requirements of fault diagnosis. We refer to this information as fallibility representations. To address this problem, we propose a novel log representation learning method, Bifrost. It draws inspiration from the log analysis experience of Site Reliability Engineers and meticulously designs strategies based on self-supervised contrastive learning to learn the fallibility representations of logs. Across three public systems and one industrial ML-as-a-Service system, the log representations produced by Bifrost outperform existing PLMs by average margins of 9.83% in F1 for anomaly detection, 18.28% in HR@k for root cause localization, and 20.88% in Macro-F1 for fault identification.

</details>


### [9] [Backend-Aware Graph Learning for Denoising Outcome Distributions in Quantum Program Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23211)
*Ning Ma,Jun Dai,Heng Li*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 提出了Q-BRIDGE，一种基于图学习的方法，通过图变换器和FiLM将含噪量子观测去噪，显著提升NISQ后端上量子程序测试的精确率和召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NISQ后端上的噪声干扰了量子程序测试的通过/失败判定，需要有效的噪声缓解方法以提高测试可靠性。

Method: 使用图变换器编码编译电路的图结构和物理后端信息，结合FiLM条件层整合含噪观测，输出去噪分布。

Result: 在23个IBM噪声后端和6个电路族上，Q-BRIDGE在测试缺陷检测中达到93.97%-94.90%精确率和82.50%-83.51%召回率，大幅超越基线。

Conclusion: 考虑电路图结构和后端物理特性是提高噪声感知量子程序测试可靠性的有效途径。

中文摘要：在NISQ（含噪中等规模量子）后端上测试量子程序具有挑战性，因为噪声会干扰结果分布并影响通过/失败判定。我们提出了Q-BRIDGE，一种基于图学习的方法，将含噪观测转换为适合基于预言机验证的去噪后分布。Q-BRIDGE使用图变换器架构编码经过编译的量子电路，捕获其门特征和连接性；物理后端信息与电路的逻辑结构一同编码。基于FiLM（特征线性调制）的附加条件层接收编码作为输入，并整合含噪观测以产生去噪结果。我们在23个IBM噪声后端和6个代表实际工作负载的电路族上评估了Q-BRIDGE。在第一个设置中，我们为每个后端分别训练一个Q-BRIDGE模型；在第二个设置中，我们训练一个在所有后端间共享的通用模型。在两种设置下，Q-BRIDGE在噪声缓解方面均大幅超越最先进基线。在含噪执行的测试场景中，Q-BRIDGE在检测由缺陷引起的测试失败时达到93.97%-94.90%的精确率和82.50%-83.51%的召回率，显著优于最先进基线。这些结果表明，考虑编译电路图结构和特定量子后端的物理特性是迈向更可靠的噪声感知量子程序测试的实用途径。

Original abstract: Testing quantum programs on NISQ (Noisy Intermediate-Scale Quantum) backends is challenging because the noise disturbs outcome distributions and can affect pass/fail decisions. We present Q-BRIDGE, a graph learning-based approach that converts noisy observations into denoised distributions suitable for oracle-based verification. Q-BRIDGE uses a graph transformer architecture to encode a transpiled quantum circuit, capturing the characteristics of its gates and their connectivity; the physical backend information is encoded together with the logical structure of the circuit. An additional conditioning layer, based on FiLM (Feature-Wise Linear Modulation), takes the encoding as input and integrates noisy observations to produce denoised outcomes. We evaluate Q-BRIDGE on 23 IBM noise backends and 6 circuit families representative of practical workloads. In the first setting, we train a separate Q-BRIDGE model for each backend; in the second setting, we train a single general model shared across all backends. Across both settings, Q-BRIDGE outperforms the state-of-the-art baseline in noise mitigation by a large margin. In testing scenarios with noisy executions, Q-BRIDGE achieves 93.97%-94.90% precision and 82.50%-83.51% recall in detecting bug-induced test failures, significantly outperforming the state-of-the-art baseline. These results indicate that considering the graph structure of the transpiled circuits and the physical characteristics of specific quantum backends is a practical route to more reliable noise-aware quantum program testing.

</details>


### [10] [Towards LLM-assisted High-Quality Property Generation for Solidity Smart Contracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23308)
*Muhammad Wahid,Shahzaib Khan,Mashhood Ali,Muhammad Hassan,Muhammad Naiman Jalil,Affan Rauf*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 利用LLM为智能合约生成测试属性，通过变异测试评估，发现Gemini Pro 1.5结合提示链能达到接近人类专家的平均变异分数，且在个别合约上完全匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能合约不可变导致漏洞修复困难，需要高效测试；手动编写属性耗时昂贵，LLM有望自动生成高质量属性。

Method: 使用LLM（如Gemini Pro 1.5）结合不同提示技术（零样本、少样本、提示链）为Solidity智能合约生成属性，并通过变异测试评估属性质量。

Result: 最佳配置（Gemini Pro 1.5+提示链）平均变异分数25.99%，接近人类基准31.75%；LibBit合约上达74.34%，与人类（74.83%）相当。

Conclusion: LLM能够生成高质量测试属性，某些情况下可达到专家水平，但存在合约间差异，仍需改进以稳定匹配人类表现。

中文摘要：智能合约的不可变特性使得一旦部署到区块链上，修复和修补漏洞变得具有挑战性。这意味着安全漏洞可能在更长时间内暴露于可能的利用，因此需要全面的部署前测试。基于属性的测试与模糊测试相结合已被证明是一种有前景的漏洞发现技术。传统上，系统属性由人类专家编写，这耗时且因此昂贵。随着大型语言模型（LLM）的最新进展及其“理解”自然语言和代码语义的能力，可能能够生成有效的属性。本研究利用最先进的LLM为基于Solidity的智能合约生成高质量属性。我们使用变异测试来衡量生成属性的质量。结果表明，LLM具有生成接近人类专家编写的高质量属性的潜力。我们广泛评估了使用各种提示技术（例如零样本、少样本和提示链）的LLM。总体而言，我们发现Gemini Pro 1.5与提示链相结合，在所有研究的配置中实现了最高的平均变异分数25.99%，接近人类编写的基准31.75%。然而，我们的逐合约分析揭示了显著差异，特别是对于LibBit合约，Gemini Pro 1.5在提示链下实现了74.34%的变异分数，与人类编写的属性（74.83%）相当。这突显了虽然平均性能具有参考价值，但单个合约级别的结果表明LLM在某些情况下可以达到专家级别的属性生成。

Original abstract: The immutable nature of smart contracts makes it challenging to fix and patch bugs once they are deployed to a blockchain. This implies that security vulnerabilities may be exposed to possible exploitation for a longer period, necessitating comprehensive pre-deployment testing. Property-based testing combined with fuzzing has proven itself as a promising technique for uncovering vulnerabilities. Traditionally, system properties are written by human experts, which is time-consuming and consequently expensive.With the recent advancement in Large Language Models (LLMs) and their ability to 'understand' natural language and code semantics, it may be possible to generate effective properties. This study, leverages state-of-the-art LLMs to generate high-quality properties for Soliditybased smart contracts. We measure the quality of the generated properties using mutation testing. Our results show that LLMs have the potential to generate high-quality properties that are close to those written by human experts. We extensively evaluate LLMs using various prompting techniques (e.g., zero shot, few shot, and prompt chaining). Overall, we find that Gemini Pro 1.5, when combined with prompt chaining, achieves the highest average mutation score of 25.99% among all studied configurations, closely approaching the human written benchmark of 31.75%. However, our per contract analysis reveals notable variance, particularly for the LibBit contract, where Gemini Pro 1.5 under prompt chaining achieves a mutation score of 74.34%, which is on par with human written properties (74.83%). This highlights that while average performance is informative, individual contract level results demonstrate that LLMs can, in some cases, match expert level property generation.

</details>


### [11] [Enhancing Code Understanding for Impact Analysis by Combining Transformers and Program Dependence Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23355)
*Yanfu Yan,Nathan Cooper,Kevin Moran,Gabriele Bavota,Denys Poshyvanyk,Steve Rich*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: Accepted to FSE'24

DOI: [10.1145/3643770](https://doi.org/10.1145/3643770)

Author affiliations: Yanfu Yan: William & Mary / Williams (United States); Nathan Cooper: William & Mary / Williams (United States); Kevin Moran: University of Central Florida; Gabriele Bavota: Università della Svizzera italiana; Denys Poshyvanyk: William & Mary / Williams (United States); Steve Rich: Cisco Systems (United States)

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 提出结合依赖图和概念耦合的IA方法Athena及大规模基准Alexandria，在25个项目上显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IA方法依赖静态、动态或演化信息，脆弱且需昂贵分析或大量历史数据；现有基准规模小且存在混乱提交问题。

Method: 结合程序依赖图与深度代码表示的conceptual coupling，无需变更历史和执行信息，并构建了25个开源项目的精细粒度基准Alexandria。

Result: 在Alexandria基准上，Athena最佳配置达到mRR 60.32%、mAP 35.19%、HIT@10 81.48%，比基线提升约10-12%，统计显著。

Conclusion: Athena通过新颖结合依赖图和概念耦合，在IA中取得显著提升，且不需变更历史和执行信息。

中文摘要：影响分析（IA）是一项关键的软件维护任务，用于识别给定代码变更对更大软件项目的影响，旨在避免潜在的不良影响。IA是一项认知上具有挑战性的任务，涉及推理各种代码构造之间的抽象关系。鉴于其难度，研究人员已致力于自动化IA，方法主要使用耦合度指标来衡量软件项目不同部分的“连通性”。许多这些耦合度指标依赖于静态、动态或演化信息，并基于启发式方法，这些方法往往脆弱，需要昂贵的执行分析或大量的共变更历史才能准确估计影响集。在本文中，我们介绍了一种新颖的IA方法，称为Athena，它将软件系统的依赖图信息与概念耦合方法相结合，该方法利用了代码的深度表示学习的最新进展，无需变更历史和执行信息。以前的IA基准测试规模小，包含少于十个软件项目，并且受到混乱提交的影响，难以准确测量结果。因此，我们构建了一个大规模的IA基准测试，称为Alexandria，来自25个开源软件项目，利用来自错误修复的细粒度提交信息。在这个新基准测试上，我们最佳配置的方法实现了mRR、mAP和HIT@10分数分别为60.32%、35.19%和81.48%。通过各种消融和定性分析，我们表明Athena的程序依赖图和概念耦合信息的新颖组合使其在统计显著性上优于简单基线10.34%、9.55%和11.68%。

Original abstract: Impact analysis (IA) is a critical software maintenance task that identifies the effects of a given set of code changes on a larger software project with the intention of avoiding potential adverse effects. IA is a cognitively challenging task that involves reasoning about the abstract relationships between various code constructs. Given its difficulty, researchers have worked to automate IA with approaches that primarily use coupling metrics as a measure of the "connectedness" of different parts of a software project. Many of these coupling metrics rely on static, dynamic, or evolutionary information and are based on heuristics that tend to be brittle, require expensive execution analysis, or large histories of co-changes to accurately estimate impact sets.
  In this paper, we introduce a novel IA approach, called Athena, that combines a software system's dependence graph information with a conceptual coupling approach that uses advances in deep representation learning for code without the need for change histories and execution information. Previous IA benchmarks are small, containing fewer than ten software projects, and suffer from tangled commits, making it difficult to measure accurate results. Therefore, we constructed a large-scale IA benchmark, called Alexandria, from 25 open-source software projects, that utilizes fine-grained commit information from bug fixes. On this new benchmark, our best-performing approach configuration achieves mRR, mAP, and HIT@10 scores of 60.32%, 35.19%, and 81.48%, respectively. Through various ablations and qualitative analyses, we show that Athena's novel combination of program dependence graphs and conceptual coupling information leads it to outperform a simpler baseline by 10.34%, 9.55%, and 11.68% with statistical significance.

</details>


### [12] [On AI Safety and Security Technical Debt in Engineering AI-Enabled Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23365)
*Muhammad Tukur,Hayatullahi B. Adeyemo,Tao Chen,Nour Ali,Anis Zarrad,Rick Kazman,Marco Agus,Rami Bahsoon*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: 64 pages, 7 figures, 8 tables, submitted to ACM Transactions on Software Engineering and Methodology (TOSEM)

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了AI技术债务，构建了基于根本原因的分类法，并提出了AITD-MAP框架以识别和缓解AI安全与安保技术债务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统在高风险领域的广泛应用，其复杂性和动态性引入了新型工程责任（AI技术债务），这些债务可能导致维护困难、可靠性下降和安全风险，因此需要系统化的管理和缓解策略。

Method: 通过系统综述60项主要研究，识别31种AITD类型，组织成7类面向根本原因的分类法，并分析其与18个信任相关关注点（6个安全危害和12个安全漏洞）的映射关系，综合出34项可行指南（8项安全指南和26项安保指南），最后构建AITD-MAP框架。

Result: 识别出31种AITD类型，分类为7类；映射到6个安全危害和12个安全漏洞；综合出34项预防、检测和减少AITD的指南；提出了AITD-MAP框架。

Conclusion: AITD-MAP框架能够帮助AI软件工程师可视化AI安全与安保技术债务，理解其根本原因并有效缓解，从而支持风险感知的AI工程。

中文摘要：人工智能（AI）系统越来越多地部署在医疗、自动驾驶、金融和教育等高风险领域。尽管这些系统提供了强大的数据驱动和自适应能力，但它们的复杂性、快速演进以及对动态数据管道的依赖引入了新型工程责任，统称为AI技术债务（AITDs）。AITDs源于数据治理、模型实现、算法设计、架构决策、运营流程、文档实践和测试充分性等根本原因。与传统技术债务不同，许多AITDs是潜在的，并在紧密耦合的AI管道中传播，导致维护挑战、可靠性下降以及安全或风险增加。在AI信任、风险与安全管理（AI TRiSM）原则的指导下，本研究通过可信度的相互关联维度重新诠释技术债务，重点关注AI安全与安保技术债务。我们对60项主要研究进行了系统综述，识别出31种不同类型的AITD，并将其组织成一个包含七个类别的面向根本原因的分类法。分析研究了这些债务如何映射到18个与信任相关的关注点，包括6个安全危害和12个安全漏洞。为支持缓解，综述综合了34项可行指南（8项安全指南和26项安保指南），旨在预防、检测和减少AI生命周期中的AITDs。基于这些发现，我们引入了AITD-MAP，一个集成框架，将AITD分类法、质量与风险影响以及缓解策略连接成一个统一的结构，用于风险感知的AI工程。该框架旨在帮助AI软件工程师使AI安全与安保技术债务可见，理解其根本原因，并缓解其存在。

Original abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) systems are increasingly deployed in high-stakes domains such as healthcare, autonomous driving, finance, and education. While these systems offer powerful data-driven and adaptive capabilities, their complexity, rapid evolution, and dependence on dynamic data pipelines introduce new forms of engineering liability collectively referred to as AI Technical Debts (AITDs). AITDs arise from root causes spanning data governance, model implementation, algorithm design, architectural decisions, operational processes, documentation practices, and testing adequacy. Unlike conventional technical debt, many AITDs are latent and propagate across tightly coupled AI pipelines, leading to maintenance challenges, reliability degradation, and heightened safety or security risks. Guided by the principles of AI Trust, Risk, and Security Management (AI TRiSM), this study reinterprets technical debt through the interconnected dimensions of trustworthiness, focusing on AI safety and security technical debts. We conduct a systematic review of 60 primary studies and identify 31 distinct types of AITD, which are organized into a root-cause-oriented taxonomy comprising seven classes. The analysis examines how these debts map to 18 trust-related concerns, including 6 safety hazards and 12 security vulnerabilities. To support mitigation, the review synthesizes 34 actionable guidelines (8 safety and 26 security) targeting the prevention, detection, and reduction of AITDs across the AI lifecycle. Building on these findings, we introduce AITD-MAP, an integrated framework that connects the AITD taxonomy, quality and risk impacts, and mitigation strategies into a unified structure for risk-aware AI engineering. The framework aims to assist AI software engineers in making AI safety and security technical debts visible, understanding their root causes, and mitigating their presence.

</details>


### [13] [TLA$^{+}$-Bench: An Execution-Grounded Benchmark and Dataset for Natural-Language to TLA+ Specification Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23425)
*Arslan Bisharat,Eric Spencer,Brian Ortiz,Khushboo Bhadauria,Mujtaba Nazari,Beatriz Santos,Anisa Ramos,TaiNing Wang,George K. Thiruvathukal,Konstantin Läufer,Mohammed Abuhamad*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: 17 pages, appendix included. Introduces TLA+-Bench, an execution-grounded benchmark and dataset for natural-language to TLA$^{+}$ specification generation. Dataset, evaluation code, and model outputs available at publication

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 提出了TLA$^{+}$-Bench数据集，通过模型检查评估TLA$^{+}$规约的正确性，发现正确率因评估选择的不同而变化很大（可达11倍），并定义了“正确率包络”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估TLA$^{+}$生成的方法（如基于相似度或可解析性）无法衡量规约的正确性，需要一种基于执行（模型检查）的精确评估手段。

Method: 构建了包含403条经模型检查的金标准和897条仅语法正确的银标准规约的数据集，附带配置，使用TLA$^{+}$模型检查器在全部可达状态空间上运行以判定正确性。还生成了四种模型编写的自然语言描述，并标注了难度和类别。

Result: 正确率范围从10.0%到1.7%（六倍变化），加入接口信息后范围扩大至18.7%到1.7%（十一倍变化）。最强模型正确率为16%（默认）和26%（给定接口名），开源模型最高1%，正确率随难度下降。

Conclusion: TLA$^{+}$生成的正确性评估高度依赖于未明确的评分选择，导致结果不稳定；提出了“正确率包络”概念来量化这一范围，并指出有效率和正确率之间存在巨大差距。

中文摘要：大型语言模型越来越多地根据自然语言描述编写TLA$^{+}$形式规约，但进展难以衡量：现有资源通过相似度或输出是否可解析来评分，但两者均不能显示正确性。我们提出TLA$^{+}$-Bench，一个通过执行来评分的数据集和基准。每条金标准规约附带一个配置，TLA$^{+}$模型检查器会在全可达状态空间上运行该配置，精确判定该规约是否满足配置所命名的属性。该数据集包含来自13个公共仓库的403条经模型检查的金标准规约和897条仅可解析的银标准规约，涵盖了先前的TLA$^{+}$生成数据，并搭载了两个提供商以两种风格编写的四条模型描述，附有难度和类别标签。我们的主要发现关乎度量本身：一个精确的判优器给出的不是一个正确率数值，而是一个范围。改变先前基准未明确说明的评分选择，在固定的一组模型输出上，正确率变化六倍，从10.0%下降到1.7%；加上接口提供选择（即告知模型配置的名称），范围扩大至十一倍，从18.7%下降到1.7%。我们将这一范围称为正确率包络，并测量了其每个边界。包络内的发现是稳定的。每个模型写出有效TLA$^{+}$的频率远高于正确TLA$^{+}$：最强模型默认情况下正确率为16%，给定接口名称时为26%，开源模型最多为1%，且正确率随难度急剧下降。

Original abstract: Large language models increasingly write TLA$^{+}$ formal specifications from natural-language descriptions, but progress is hard to measure: existing resources grade by resemblance to a reference or by whether the output parses, neither of which shows correctness. We present TLA$^{+}$-Bench, a dataset and benchmark that grades by execution. Every gold specification ships a configuration the TLA$^{+}$ model checker runs over the full reachable state space, deciding exactly whether the specification holds the properties that configuration names. The dataset holds 403 model-checked gold and 897 parse-only silver specifications from 13 public repositories, subsumes prior TLA$^{+}$ generation data, and carries four model-written descriptions in two styles from two providers, with difficulty and category labels. Our main finding is about measurement itself: an exact oracle gives not one correctness number but a range. Varying only the grading choices earlier benchmarks leave unstated, on one fixed set of model outputs, the correct rate moves sixfold, from 10.0\% to 1.7\%; adding the interface-supply choice, where the model is told the configuration's names, widens the range to elevenfold, from 18.7\% to 1.7\%. We call this range the correctness envelope and measure each of its bounds. The findings inside it are stable. Every model writes valid TLA$^{+}$ far more often than correct TLA$^{+}$: the strongest is correct 16\% of the time by default and 26\% when given the interface names, open models at most 1\%, and correctness falls sharply with difficulty.

</details>


### [14] [LinkRank: A Learning-to-Rank Framework for One-to-Many Issue-Commit Traceability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23610)
*Abhishek Kumar,Tuhin Mondal,Partha Pratim Das,Partha Pratim Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: LinkRank是一个学习排序框架，通过迭代选择提交来解决一对多问题-提交链接恢复，在六个项目中显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法假设一对一关系，忽略实际中一个问题对应多个提交的情况，导致不完整的可追溯性。

Method: LinkRank采用学习排序方法，考虑问题候选提交集合，通过迭代选择-移除-重新归一化策略识别最可能解决该问题的提交。

Result: 在已知K设置下平均F1达74.54%，远超基线48.39%；未知K设置下ABS和REL规则分别达68.84%和67.02% F1。

Conclusion: 一对多问题-提交追溯更适合作为以问题为中心的排序和迭代选择问题，而非独立成对分类。

中文摘要：恢复问题（issue）与提交（commit）之间的可追溯链接对于软件维护、调试、影响分析和项目理解至关重要。然而，大多数现有方法假设一对一关系，即每个问题链接到单个提交。在实践中，许多问题是通过多个提交解决的，忽略这种一对多关系可能导致不完整的可追溯性。本文提出了LinkRank，一种用于恢复一对多问题-提交链接的学习排序（learning-to-rank）框架。与主要独立判断问题-提交对的现有方法不同，LinkRank考虑一个问题的候选提交集合，并识别出最有可能解决该问题的提交。为了支持现实评估，我们从六个开源GitHub仓库构建了一个新数据集。LinkRank遵循迭代的“选择-移除-重新归一化”策略：选择排名最高的提交，将其从候选池中移除，重新归一化剩余分数，并重复该过程直到满足停止标准。我们在两种设置下评估LinkRank：已知K（已知链接提交的真实数量）和未知K（模型必须使用ABS和REL停止规则推断何时停止选择提交）。在六个项目中，LinkRank在已知K设置下平均F1得分为74.54%，而最强基线为48.39%。在未知K设置下，LinkRank在ABS和REL自动停止规则下分别达到68.84%和67.02%的F1得分，均优于最强基线。总体而言，研究结果表明，一对多问题-提交追溯更适合作为以问题为中心的排序和迭代选择问题，而不是独立的成对分类。

Original abstract: Recovering traceability links between issues and commits is important for software maintenance, debugging, impact analysis, and project understanding. However, most existing approaches assume a one-to-one relationship, where each issue is linked to a single commit. In practice, many issues are resolved through multiple commits, and ignoring this one-to-many nature can lead to incomplete traceability. This paper presents LinkRank, a learning-to-rank framework for recovering one-to-many issue--commit links. Unlike existing methods that mainly judge issue--commit pairs independently, LinkRank considers the set of candidate commits for an issue and identifies the commits that are most likely to contribute to its resolution. To support realistic evaluation, we construct a new dataset from six open-source GitHub repositories. LinkRank follows an iterative pick--remove--renormalize strategy: it selects the highest-ranked commit, removes it from the candidate pool, renormalizes the remaining scores, and repeats the process until the stopping criterion is met. We evaluate LinkRank under two settings: Known-K, where the true number of linked commits is available, and Unknown-K, where the model must infer when to stop selecting commits using ABS and REL stopping rules. Across six projects, LinkRank achieves an average Known-K F1 score of 74.54%, compared with 48.39% for the strongest baseline. In the Unknown-K setting, LinkRank achieves 68.84% F1 with ABS and 67.02% F1 with REL, outperforming the strongest baselines under both automatic stopping rules. Overall, the findings suggest that one-to-many issue--commit traceability is better addressed as an issue-centric ranking and iterative selection problem than as independent pairwise classification.

</details>


### [15] [Where Is the Cost of Third-Party API Routers in Agentic Software Development?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23624)
*Donghao Fu,Jingxin Li,Xue Jiang,Yihong Dong*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 本文实证研究了编码代理中第三方API路由器端注入攻击，发现现有客户端防御完全失效（防御成功率0%），提出SIDEL框架并评估四种注入级别，呼吁提供商端输出完整性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第三方API路由器位于代理和LLM提供商之间，可篡改数据但缺乏验证机制，其实际安全影响未经验证。

Method: 设计了四种递增隐蔽性的路由器端注入（L1-L4），开发SIDEL框架进行记录、重放和评估，在400样本数据集上测试四个编码代理及两种防御措施。

Result: 所有代理在所有注入级别下防御成功率为0%；客户端防御（白名单、LLM审查）提高抵抗力但未完全恢复控制。

Conclusion: 现有客户端防护不足以应对路由器端注入，需提供商端输出完整性保证，如签名或硬件锚定。

中文摘要：第三方API路由器已成为一个通用层，统一了对日益多样化的LLM提供商的访问。在编码代理工作流中，高自主性操作被广泛采用，因为它减少了交互开销。因此，位于代理和上游提供商之间的第三方API路由器不可避免地占据了可信路径。它可以检查和修改每个请求和响应，但没有机制验证提供商的输出与代理最终执行的仓库级操作之间的一致性。因此，客户端权限机制在实践中可能变得无效。这种控制差距是否对软件开发任务产生真实且难以检测的影响，尚缺乏实证测量。在本文中，我们对编码代理中的路由器端注入进行了实证研究，考察了四个逐渐微妙的干预级别：响应替换（L1）、响应追加（L2）、LLM精炼注入（L3）和LLM精炼与分布对齐注入（L4）。此外，我们开发了SIDEL框架，用于跟踪记录、重放、注入和防御评估，并策划了包含400个样本的数据集。我们评估了四个代表性编码代理，并进一步评估了基于白名单的执行控制和LLM审查。路由器端干预显著改变了仓库级操作，并且现有客户端防护措施难以检测。在没有额外缓解措施的情况下，所有评估的代理在所有注入级别上的防御成功率为0%。客户端缓解措施和反应式审查提高了抵抗力，但并未完全恢复端到端控制，这促使了提供商端输出完整性保证的需求。我们的代码可在https://github.com/Riyasushin/SIDE获取。

Original abstract: Third-party API routers have become a common layer that unifies access across increasingly diverse LLM providers. In coding-agent workflows, high-autonomy operation is widely adopted because it reduces interaction overhead. As a result, a third-party API router, which sits between the agent and the upstream provider, inevitably occupies the trusted path. It can inspect and modify every request and response, yet no mechanism verifies alignment between the provider's output and the repository-level actions ultimately executed by the agent. Consequently, client-side permission mechanisms may become ineffective in practice. Whether this control gap produces real, hard-to-detect effects on software development tasks remains empirically unmeasured. In this paper, we conduct an empirical study of router-side injection in coding agents, examining four intervention levels of increasing subtlety: Response Substitution (L1), Response Append (L2), LLM-Polished Injection (L3), and LLM-Polished with Distribution Alignment Injection (L4). Moreover, we develop SIDEL, a framework for trace recording, replay, injection, and defense evaluation, with a curated dataset of 400 samples. We evaluate four representative coding agents, and further evaluate whitelist-based execution control and LLM review. Router-side intervention substantially alters repository-level actions and remains difficult for existing client-side safeguards to detect. Without additional mitigations, all evaluated agents achieved a defense success rate of 0 percent across all injection levels. Client-side mitigations and reactive reviews improve resistance but do not fully restore end-to-end control, motivating provider-side output-integrity guarantees. Our code is available at https://github.com/Riyasushin/SIDE.

</details>


### [16] [Multi-level Code Optimization via Mixture of Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23665)
*Yun Peng,Jun Wan,Jiakun Liu,Shuzheng Gao,David Lo,Xiaoxue Ren*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: This paper has been accepted by ASE 2026

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: Optimo是一种基于LLM和混合提示架构的多级代码优化方法，通过差异分析识别瓶颈并应用多级优化，在两种基准上显著提升代码效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM优化方法无法识别合适的优化目标且仅进行单级优化，传统方法难以处理动态语言，因此需要更有效的多级优化方法。

Method: 提出Optimo，基于Mixture-of-Prompts架构，通过差异分析识别时间关键代码结构，路由到不同优化策略，在四个抽象级别（算法改进到API优化）进行优化。

Result: 在COFFE和Effibench上，对人类编写代码达到57.48% opt%和3.97倍加速，对LLM生成代码达到42.42% opt%和13.51倍加速，opt%优于基线最高96.51%。

Conclusion: Optimo通过多级LLM优化有效提升动态语言代码效率，性能显著优于现有方法。

中文摘要：运行时效率是影响软件质量和用户满意度的关键因素。有许多方法被提出来优化代码以提高运行时效率。传统的代码优化方法在编译期间对静态语言的中级表示（IR）进行操作。它们是有效的，但难以处理不需要编译的动态语言。最近，大型语言模型（LLM）已被用于直接优化动态语言的源代码。然而，这些方法无法识别合适的优化目标，并且通常进行不全面的单级优化。为了解决这些挑战，我们提出了Optimo，一种基于新型混合提示（MoP）架构的多级LLM代码优化方法。在MoP架构中，Optimo通过差异分析识别时间关键的代码结构作为性能瓶颈。然后，这些结构被路由到一些优化策略，类似于MoE中的专家模型，每个策略针对特定代码模式进行优化。与传统方法仅关注语句级优化不同，Optimo在四个抽象级别上操作，从粗粒度的算法改进到细粒度的API使用优化。我们在两个代码效率基准测试COFFE和Effibench上评估了Optimo。我们的结果表明，Optimo实现了高达57.48%的opt%（即优化后程序正确且比原始程序快至少10%的百分比），在优化人类编写代码时实现了高达3.97倍的加速，并且在opt%上始终优于最佳基线，最高达96.51%。此外，Optimo在优化LLM生成的代码时实现了高达42.42%的opt%和高达13.51倍的加速。

Original abstract: Runtime efficiency is a critical factor that impacts both software quality and user satisfaction. There are many approaches proposed for code optimization to improve runtime efficiency. Traditional code optimization methods operate on intermediate representations (IRs) during compilation for static languages. They are effective but struggle to handle dynamic languages that do not require compilation. Recently, large language models (LLMs) have been leveraged to directly optimize source code in dynamic languages. However, these methods fail to identify suitable optimization targets and usually conduct incomprehensive single-level optimization.
  To address these challenges, we propose Optimo, a multi-level LLM-based code optimization approach built on a novel Mixture-of-Prompts (MoP) architecture. In the MoP architecture, Optimo identifies time-critical code structures as performance bottlenecks via differential profiling. These structures are then routed to some optimization strategies, akin to expert models in MoE, each tailored to optimize specific code patterns. Unlike traditional approaches that focus only on statement-level optimizations, Optimo operates at four levels of abstraction, ranging from coarse-grained algorithmic improvements to fine-grained optimizations in API usage. We evaluate Optimo on two code efficiency benchmarks, COFFE and Effibench. Our results demonstrate that Optimo achieves an up to 57.48% opt%, i.e., the percentage of optimized programs that are correct and at least 10% faster than the original programs, and an up to 3.97x speedup when optimizing human-written code, and it consistently outperforms the best baseline by up to 96.51% in terms of opt%. Furthermore, Optimo achieves an up to 42.42% opt% and an up to 13.51x speedup when optimizing LLM-generated code.

</details>


### [17] [Semantic-Enhanced Automatic Refinement of Architecture Recovery Results Using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23774)
*Yiran Zhang,Chengwei Liu,Yuqiang Sun,Zhengzi Xu,Weisong Sun,Wenke Li,Wuxia Jin,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: Accepted by ICSE 2026

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: Semref结合LLM和依赖分析，自动提升现有架构恢复工具的准确性，在多个项目上实现最高43.35%的归一化增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动架构恢复工具准确性有限，架构师仍需大量手动完善恢复结果，亟需一种自动细化方法来减少人工工作量。

Method: 提出Semref框架，结合大语言模型的语义理解能力和依赖分析，自动完善已有架构恢复工具的输出。

Result: 在9个项目上使用5个指标评估，归一化增益17.72%–43.35%，MoJoFM和a2a_adj指标相对提升118.57%和100.41%。

Conclusion: Semref能有效提高架构恢复的准确性，显著减少人工完善工作量。

中文摘要：理解架构对于有效维护和管理大型软件系统至关重要。然而，设计架构与实现架构之间常常存在差异，这可能带来重大风险。为了识别这些差异，架构师需要从系统实现中提取架构，这一过程既耗时又容易出错。为简化这一过程，许多自动架构恢复技术已被开发出来。然而，它们的准确性往往有限。架构师仍需投入大量精力来完善恢复结果，以确保其准确反映实现的架构。为了减少这种手动工作，我们引入了Semref，这是一个结合LLM与依赖分析的框架，用于自动完善现有架构恢复工具恢复的架构。通过利用LLM的语义理解能力并整合结构依赖关系，Semref提高了恢复架构的准确性和可理解性。为了评估Semref，我们在9个具有公开真实架构的项目和10个最先进的架构恢复工具上进行了测试。采用5个常用指标来评估Semref的有效性。结果表明，Semref在各种指标上提高了准确性，归一化增益范围从17.72%到43.35%。具体而言，对于MoJoFM和a2a_adj指标，Semref分别实现了118.57%和100.41%的相对改进。

Original abstract: Understanding the architecture is crucial for effectively maintaining and managing large software systems. However, discrepancies often exist between the designed and implemented architectures, which can pose significant risks. To identify these discrepancies, architects need to extract the architecture from the system implementation, which is both time-consuming and error-prone. To simplify this procedure, many automatic architecture recovery techniques have been developed. Yet, their accuracy is often limited. Architects must still invest significant effort in refining recovery results to ensure they accurately reflect the implemented architecture.
  To reduce such manual effort, we introduce Semref, a framework that combines LLMs with dependency analysis to automatically refine architectures recovered by existing architecture recovery tools. By leveraging the LLM's semantic understanding capabilities and integrating structural dependencies, Semref enhances both the accuracy and the comprehension of recovered architectures. To evaluate Semref, we tested on 9 projects with published ground-truth architectures and 10 state-of-the-art architecture recovery tools. 5 commonly used metrics are adopted to evaluate the effectiveness of Semref. The results show that Semref improves accuracy across various metrics, with normalized gains ranges from 17.72\% to 43.35\%. Specifically, for MoJoFM and $a2a_{adj}$ metrics, Semref achieves relative improvements of 118.57\% and 100.41\%, respectively.

</details>


### [18] [The Influence of Fraudulent AI-Generated Responses on Software Engineering Surveys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23805)
*Ronnie de Souza Santos,Italo Santos,Maria Teresa Baldassarre,Cleyton Magalhaes,Mairieli Wessel*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 本文研究AI辅助响应如何影响软件工程调查结果的有效性，发现定量结果相对稳定但定性结果更易受影响，强调多重验证的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型可能被用于生成AI辅助的虚假响应，影响软件工程调查的有效性，但影响程度未知。

Method: 对四个软件工程调查数据集进行二次分析，包括人工识别可疑响应、AI检测工具、描述性统计和主题分析，比较原始与清理后的结果。

Result: 定量结果在过滤可疑响应后总体稳定，但定性结果受较大影响；人口统计和上下文解释有变化。

Conclusion: AI辅助响应对定量和定性分析的影响不同，研究建议对开放式响应进行多重验证。

中文摘要：背景：大型语言模型（LLMs）引发了关于软件工程调查中存在欺诈或AI辅助参与的新担忧。目的：本研究调查可疑或可能AI辅助的响应如何影响软件工程调查结果的有效性。方法：我们对四个软件工程调查数据集进行了二次分析，采用人工识别可疑响应、自动化AI生成文本检测、描述性统计分析和主题分析。我们比较了原始数据集和人工清理后数据集的结果。结果：在过滤可疑响应后，定量结果总体保持稳定，尽管一些人口统计学和分析变量显示出中等变化，影响了对特定参与者群体和背景特征的解释。相比之下，定性结果受到情境框架变化、代码显著性以及支持解释的证据性质的影响更大，从而塑造了参与者经历和研究背景的解释和表征方式。结论：AI辅助参与可能根据所进行的分析类型不同而对软件工程调查结果产生不同影响。这些发现强化了结合多种验证程序的重要性，特别是在依赖开放式响应的研究中。

Original abstract: Background: Large Language Models (LLMs) introduce new concerns regarding fraudulent or AI assisted participation in software engineering surveys. Aims: This study investigates how suspicious or potentially AI assisted responses may affect the validity of software engineering survey findings. Method: We conducted a secondary analysis of four software engineering survey datasets using manual identification of suspicious responses, automated AI generated text detection, descriptive statistical analysis, and thematic analysis. We compared findings obtained from the original and manually cleaned datasets. Results: Quantitative findings generally remained stable after filtering suspicious responses, although some demographic and analytical variables showed moderate variation, affecting the interpretation of specific participant groups and contextual characteristics. In contrast, qualitative findings were more strongly influenced by changes in contextual framing, code prominence, and the nature of the evidence supporting interpretation, shaping how participants' experiences and study contexts were interpreted and characterized. Conclusions: AI assisted participation may influence software engineering survey findings differently depending on the type of analysis being conducted. The findings reinforce the importance of combining multiple validation procedures, particularly in studies relying on open ended responses.

</details>


### [19] [RESTOR: Automated Test Oracle Generation for RESTful APIs via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23963)
*Xun Zhou,Zhen Dong,Mingyu Ren,Qiang Li,JunJie Li,Sifan Wang,Xiaolong Yu,Chaofeng Sha,Xin Peng*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: Accepted to ISSTA 2026. 22 pages, 8 figures, 2 tables

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: Restor通过单次请求-响应对生成测试断言，利用GRPO微调轻量LLM，在工业数据集上达到85.42% F1分数，并在生产中将测试用例采纳率从74.1%提升至96%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有REST API测试在缺乏正式规范和历史日志时难以定义可靠预言，尤其是新部署端点。

Method: 提出Restor框架，使用数据增强和GRPO微调轻量LLM，优化奖励函数以选择稳定字段并生成鲁棒断言。

Result: 在超过2300个API轨迹上，关键字段识别F1达85.42%，生产部署后测试用例采纳率从74.1%升至96%以上。

Conclusion: Restor有效解决了单次流量下的测试预言生成问题，显著提升自动化测试采纳率并降低QA工作量。

中文摘要：现代REST API测试面临一个关键挑战：如何定义可靠的测试预言，特别是在敏捷工业环境中，正式规范（如OpenAPI）经常缺失或过时，且新部署的端点没有历史执行日志。在本文中，我们提出了Restor（面向REST API的强化增强单流量预言生成器），这是一个框架，能够在黑盒设置中从单个观察到的请求-响应对生成可执行的测试断言。与依赖基于规则的模板或大量训练日志的现有方法不同，Restor利用新颖的数据增强流水线，通过组相对策略优化（GRPO）微调轻量级大语言模型（LLM）。这一训练过程使模型能够内化测试“常识”，通过优化奖励函数，该函数共同鼓励：（i）选择稳定、语义有意义的字段进行验证，避免动态噪声（如时间戳或跟踪ID）；（ii）生成能够承受逻辑变化的鲁棒断言。我们在一个包含超过2300个API轨迹（涵盖246个真实世界服务）的工业数据集上评估了Restor。综合实验表明，Restor显著优于基于提示工程的基线和通用模型，在关键字段识别上实现了85.42%的优越F1分数，并提高了语义准确断言的比例。此外，在字节跳动生产CI/CD工作流中的部署证实了其实用价值：该系统将自动生成测试用例的采纳率从74.1%提升至超过96%，在保证高执行稳定性的同时大幅减少了手动质量保证（QA）工作。

Original abstract: Modern REST API testing faces a critical challenge in defining reliable test oracles, particularly in agile industrial environments where formal specifications (e.g., OpenAPI) are frequently missing or outdated, and historical execution logs are unavailable for newly deployed endpoints. In this paper, we present Restor (Reinforcement Enhanced Single-Traffic Oracle generator for REST APIs), a framework that generates executable test assertions from a single observed request-response pair in a black-box setting. Unlike existing approaches that rely on rule-based templates or massive training logs, Restor utilizes a novel data augmentation pipeline to fine-tune a lightweight Large Language Model (LLM) via Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). This training process enables the model to internalize testing "common sense" by optimizing a reward function that jointly encourages: (i) the selection of stable, semantically meaningful fields for validation and the avoidance of dynamic noise (e.g., timestamps or trace IDs); (ii) the generation of robust assertions that withstand logic variations. We evaluate Restor on an industrial dataset comprising over 2,300 API traces across 246 real-world services. Comprehensive experiments demonstrate that Restor significantly outperforms prompt-engineered baselines and generalist models, achieving a superior $F_1$ score of 85.42% in key field identification and increasing the proportion of semantically accurate assertions. Furthermore, deployment in a production CI/CD workflow at ByteDance confirms its practical value: the system raised the adoption rate of automatically generated test cases from 74.1% to over 96%, substantially reducing manual Quality Assurance (QA) effort while ensuring high execution stability.

</details>


### [20] [Industrial Practice of LLM-Based Test Case Carving and Assertion Generation (Experience Paper)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24000)
*Haozhen You,Zhen Dong,Jingjing Wang,Qiang Li,Xin Peng*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: 23 pages, 6 figures, Proceedings of the 35th ACM SIGSOFT International Symposium on Software Testing and Analysis (ISSTA 2026)

DOI: [10.1145/3832099](https://doi.org/10.1145/3832099)

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: NL2Test利用自然语言描述和流量捕获，通过LLM和确定性算法自动生成可执行的API回归测试，在工业场景中达到82.4%的精确匹配率和85.4%的代码采纳率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微服务回归测试常受文档不完整限制，手工将流量转化为可重放测试耗时且易错。

Method: NL2Test结合自然语言场景描述和流量捕获，使用LLM进行语义解释和约束代码合成，并通过确定性算法进行请求过滤、依赖确认和断言路径验证，完成测试用例提取和断言生成。

Result: 在51个工业场景中，精确匹配率82.4%，功能可用率98.0%；9个月部署生成3196个测试用例，代码采纳率85.4%。

Conclusion: 基于流量生成并加入确定性约束的方法能显著减少人工工作，提升微服务回归自动化水平。

中文摘要：企业回归测试对于微服务系统而言，常常受到不完整或过时文档的限制。在实践中，QA工程师经常依赖真实执行流量来重建业务场景；然而，将原始流量转化为可重放的回归测试并具有稳定的验证逻辑，仍然是劳动密集且容易出错的。本文提出了NL2Test，一种端到端的方法和工具，它从（i）自然语言场景描述和（ii）执行该场景时记录的流量捕获中生成可执行的API回归测试。NL2Test解决了两个耦合的任务：测试用例提取（从流量中提取最小的可重放请求序列并重建数据依赖关系，使得动态值从其响应中绑定而不是硬编码）和断言生成（生成与业务意图一致的断言，同时避免非确定性字段和幻觉路径）。为了提高可靠性，NL2Test使用LLM进行语义解释和受约束的代码合成，并使用确定性算法进行请求过滤、通过值一致性进行依赖确认以及断言路径验证。我们在从一家大型面向消费者的互联网公司提取的51个工业回归场景上评估了NL2Test。NL2Test实现了82.4%（42/51）的精确匹配率，并且在允许轻微后期编辑的情况下，在98.0%（50/51）的场景中产生了功能可用的草稿。在2025年3月开始的9个月生产部署中，NL2Test生成了3,196个测试用例，整体代码采纳率为85.4%。这些结果表明，具有确定性护栏的流量驱动生成可以显著减少手动工作，同时提高复杂微服务环境中的回归自动化。

Original abstract: Enterprise regression testing for microservice systems is often constrained by incomplete or outdated documentation. In practice, QA engineers frequently rely on real execution traffic to reconstruct business scenarios; however, turning raw traffic into replayable regression tests with stable validation logic remains labor-intensive and error-prone.
  This paper presents NL2Test, an end-to-end approach and tool that generates executable API regression tests from (i) a natural-language scenario description and (ii) a traffic capture recorded while executing the scenario. NL2Test addresses two coupled tasks: test case carving, which extracts a minimal replayable request sequence and reconstructs data dependencies so that dynamic values are bound from their responses rather than hard-coded; and assertion generation, which produces assertions aligned with business intent while avoiding non-deterministic fields and hallucinated paths. To improve reliability, NL2Test uses LLMs for semantic interpretation and constrained code synthesis, and uses deterministic algorithms for request filtering, dependency confirmation via value consistency, and assertion-path validation.
  We evaluate NL2Test on 51 industrial regression scenarios extracted from a large consumer-facing Internet company. NL2Test achieves an exact-match rate of 82.4% (42/51), and produces a functionally usable draft in 98.0% (50/51) of scenarios when allowing minor post-edits. In a 9-month production deployment starting in March 2025, NL2Test generated 3,196 test cases with an overall code adoption rate of 85.4%. These results indicate that traffic-grounded generation with deterministic guardrails can substantially reduce manual effort while improving regression automation in complex microservice environments.

</details>


### [21] [Four Years of GenAI: How Educators and Industry Adapted Their Assessment Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24246)
*May Mahmoud,Nisa Shahid,Izah Sohail,Gulshan Sharma,Hanan Salam,Sarah Nadi*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 通过调查教育者和招聘者，发现GenAI正在改变初级软件工程师的技能评估，但双方在毕业生准备度上存在分歧，尤其是在基础非GenAI技能方面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GenAI的快速发展改变了软件工程任务，需要重新定义初级软件工程师的核心技能及其评估方式。

Method: 采用两项调查，分别针对56名教育工作者和24名招聘专业人士，收集并分析他们对GenAI影响下技能和评估的看法。

Result: 教育者侧重学术诚信，行业普遍使用GenAI；双方都重视批判性评估AI输出、负责任使用GenAI工具和适应能力，但招聘者认为毕业生在这些技能上准备不足，尤其是基础非GenAI技能。

Conclusion: 教育和招聘应调整策略，弥合双方在毕业生技能准备度上的差距，并发展抗GenAI的评估方法。

中文摘要：GenAI 解决广泛软件工程任务的能力正在重塑软件行业，引发了一个问题：如今的初级软件工程师应该是什么样？在这项工作中，我们调查了初级工程师的核心技能是否因 GenAI 而改变，以及这些技能的评估方式如何变化。通过两次调查，我们从教育和招聘两个角度进行考量，基于来自不同地理区域的 56 位教育工作者和 24 位招聘专业人士的回应，以识别差距和错位的期望。我们发现，教育工作者主要围绕学术诚信来制定 GenAI 政策，而行业中对 GenAI 的使用几乎是普遍的，组织政策规定的是如何使用而非是否使用。面试中对 GenAI 使用的政策仍在形成中，受访者对于是否偏好展示 GenAI 技能的候选人意见不一。两个群体都在转向基于可观察的实时交互和更高层次任务的抗 GenAI 评估，并且他们都同意哪些技能最重要，高度重视对 AI 生成输出的批判性评估、负责任且有效地使用 GenAI 工具，以及独立学习和适应的能力。然而，他们在毕业生对这些技能的 readiness 上存在分歧：招聘专业人士报告了更大的差距，这种分歧集中在基础的、非 GenAI 特定的技能上。

Original abstract: GenAI's ability to solve a wide range of software engineering tasks is reshaping the software industry, raising the question of what an entry-level software engineer looks like today. In this work, we investigate whether the core skills for entry-level engineers have changed with GenAI and how the assessment of those skills has changed. Through two surveys, we consider both the educational and hiring perspectives, drawing on responses from 56 educators and 24 hiring professionals across diverse geographic regions to identify gaps and misaligned expectations. We find that educators frame their GenAI policies primarily around academic integrity, whereas GenAI use in industry is near-universal, with organizational policy governing how it should be used rather than whether to use it. Policies on GenAI use during interviews are still emerging, and respondents are split on whether they prefer candidates who demonstrate GenAI skills. Both populations are moving toward GenAI-resistant assessments built on observable real-time interactions and higher-order tasks, and both agree on which skills matter most, rating critical evaluation of AI-generated output, responsible and effective use of GenAI tools, and the ability to learn and adapt independently highly. However, they disagree on graduate readiness in these skills: hiring professionals report larger gaps, with the disagreement concentrated on foundational, non-GenAI-specific skills.

</details>


### [22] [Alternative UX Extensions and Their Trade-offs for Code Completion in Pharo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24253)
*Mehdi Elkolei,Omar Abedelkader,Stéphane Ducasse*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 本文为Complishon代码补全引擎添加了容错、隐式前缀扩展、分组补全条目和驼峰匹配四个扩展，在保持模块化架构的同时解决了现有限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Complishon引擎的前缀匹配对打字错误敏感、框架前缀导致冗余输入、大型菜单难以扫描且不支持驼峰缩写，需要扩展来解决这些实际限制。

Method: 通过实现四个扩展：容错、隐式前缀扩展、分组补全条目和驼峰匹配，每个扩展都描述了实现细节、替代设计及权衡，并保持模块化架构。

Result: 四个扩展成功集成到Complishon中，解决了严格前缀匹配的局限性，提升了补全的鲁棒性和可用性。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是展示了这些改进可以在不破坏模块化设计和可预测行为的前提下集成到Complishon中。

中文摘要：Complishon是Pharo的上下文感知代码补全引擎，构建于AST分析、懒惰候选生成和基于过滤器的候选选择之上。其现有设计已提供强大的语义补全，但仍存在若干实际限制。严格的前缀匹配对微小打字错误敏感，框架前缀常强制冗余输入，大型补全菜单难以扫描，且仅前缀匹配不支持常见的驼峰缩写。本文提出了四个扩展来解决这些限制：容错、隐式前缀扩展、分组补全条目和驼峰匹配。对于每个扩展，我们描述了实现，讨论了替代设计，并解释了权衡。本文的主要贡献是表明这些改进可以集成到Complishon中，同时保持其模块化架构和可预测行为。

Original abstract: Complishon is Pharo's context-aware code completion engine, built on AST analysis, lazy candidate generation, and filter-based candidate selection. Its existing design already provides strong semantic completion, but several practical limits remain. Strict prefix matching is sensitive to small typing errors, framework prefixes often force redundant input, large completion menus are difficult to scan, and prefix-only matching does not support common camel-case abbreviations. This paper presents four extensions that address these limits: typo tolerance, implicit prefix expansion, grouped completion entries, and camel-case matching. For each extension, we describe the implementation, discuss alternative designs, and explain the trade-offs involved. The main contribution of the paper is to show that these improvements can be integrated into Complishon while preserving its modular architecture and predictable behavior.

</details>


### [23] [Motivations and Barriers to Communicating Software Engineering Research: Insights from Early Career Researchers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24387)
*Shalini Chakraborty,Marvin Wyrich,Sven Apel,Sebastian Baltes*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: 38 pages, 5 figures

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 本文通过访谈18名软件工程博士生，发现他们在科学传播中抱负与实践之间存在矛盾，面临社交焦虑、传播渠道不确定等障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学传播日益重要，但早期职业软件工程研究者如何实践科学传播尚不清楚。

Method: 对18名不同国际背景的软件工程博士生进行半结构化访谈，采用主题分析考察动机、传播渠道和障碍。

Result: 参与者动力强烈，但受社交焦虑、传播对象不明、反馈机制缺失、机构指导不足及传播渠道碎片化等因素限制，行动受阻。

Conclusion: 需要为软件工程博士生提供定制化、平衡的支持系统，帮助他们有效自信地进行科学传播。

中文摘要：科学传播正逐渐成为现代研究生涯的一部分，要求研究人员传播知识、与更广泛的社区互动，并增加其工作的社会影响。尽管其重要性日益增长，但关于早期职业软件工程研究人员在实践中的科学传播感知和导航了解甚少。在本文中，我们研究了软件工程博士生如何体验科学传播。我们对来自不同国际背景的18名博士生进行了半结构化访谈。通过主题分析，我们考察了三个相互关联的维度：动机、传播渠道和障碍。我们的发现揭示了抱负与实践之间的强烈张力。参与者非常有动力参与科学传播，因为有机会进行合作、获得专业认可、产生更广泛的影响以及为自己和他们的研究进行倡导。然而，将这些动机转化为行动常常受到社交焦虑、对合适受众和传播场所的不确定性、有限的反馈机制、不足的机构指导以及应对日益碎片化的传播格局的挑战所限制。我们的发现强调需要量身定制的、平衡的支持系统，以帮助软件工程博士生在不同文化和机构环境中有效且自信地参与科学传播。我们概述了实践意义，为未来工作解决这些挑战提供了初步指导。

Original abstract: Science communication is increasingly becoming a part of modern research careers, involving researchers to disseminate knowledge, engage broader communities, and increase the societal impact of their work. Despite its growing importance, little is known about how early-career software engineering researchers perceive and navigate science communication in practice. In this paper, we investigate how PhD students in software engineering experience science communication. We conducted semi-structured interviews with 18 doctoral candidates from diverse international backgrounds. Using thematic analysis, we examine three interconnected dimensions: motivations, communication channels, and barriers. Our findings reveal a strong tension between aspiration and practice. Participants were highly motivated to engage in science communication due to opportunities for collaboration, professional recognition, broader impact, and advocacy for themselves and their research. However, translating these motivations into action was frequently constrained by social anxiety, uncertainty regarding appropriate audiences and communication venues, limited feedback mechanisms, insufficient institutional guidance, and challenges associated with navigating an increasingly fragmented communication landscape. Our findings highlight the need for tailored, balanced support systems that empower software engineering PhD students to engage in science communication effectively and confidently across diverse cultural and institutional environments. We outline practical implications that offer initial guidance for addressing these challenges in future work.

</details>


### [24] [Evaluating the Impact of Explainable AI on Trust in AI-Assisted Code Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24601)
*Zhenhan Gao,Marvin Muñoz Barón,Umm-e Habiba,Daniel Graziotin,Stefan Wagner*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: 23 pages, 4 figures, 5 tables. To appear in Proceedings of the ACM on Software Engineering (PACMSE), Vol. 3, No. ISSTA, Article ISSTA093 (ISSTA 2026). Published under CC BY 4.0. Replication package: https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.21457282

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 通过用户实验发现，不同级别的XAI解释会影响开发者对AI代码审查的信任和认同，但并非解释越多认同越高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM用于代码审查但决策不透明，开发者难以信任，而XAI在其中的作用尚未被充分研究。

Method: 进行34名被试的内研究，比较三种XAI级别的基于LLM的代码审查系统（详细解释、仅反馈、无解释），测量信任、一致性、推理时间和原因。

Result: 完整解释信任最高（3.99/5），但中等解释一致性最高（89.22%），无解释最低；解释水平不影响审查时间；常见决策原因是代码可读性和正确性。

Conclusion: XAI显著影响信任和一致性，为设计可信赖的AI代码审查系统提供了设计依据。

中文摘要：背景：大型语言模型（LLM）越来越多地用于自动化代码审查，但其决策背后的推理仍然难以理解。开发人员难以评估LLM生成审查的有效性，因此很难判断应该对其给予多少信任。可解释人工智能（XAI）在代码审查中的作用及其对信任的影响尚未得到充分研究。目标：我们研究XAI对开发人员信任AI辅助代码审查的影响。方法：我们进行了一项包含34名参与者的被试内用户研究，比较了三种不同XAI支持级别的基于LLM的代码审查系统：条件A（详细解释和审查反馈）、条件B（仅审查反馈）和条件C（无解释）。参与者审查了真实的代码变更请求以及AI生成的审查。我们测量了信任感知、与AI建议的一致性、每个决策给出的推理以及所用时间。结果：解释水平显著影响信任和与AI建议的一致性，但方式不同。完整解释（A）产生最高的感知信任（平均值=3.99/5），但不是最高的一致性，而中等解释（B）实现了最高的一致性（89.22%）。这可能表明更多的解释促使开发人员更频繁地质疑AI建议。无解释（C）导致最低的信任和一致性。解释水平未显著影响审查时间。最常被引用的决策原因是代码可读性和正确性。结论：将XAI纳入代码审查显著改变了信任感知和与AI建议的一致性。这些结果为设计评估可信赖的基于AI的代码审查系统以及研究AI辅助软件开发中的人为因素提供了信息。

Original abstract: Background: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to automate code review, but the reasoning behind their decisions remains hard to understand. Developers struggle to assess the validity of LLM-generated reviews, making it difficult to gauge how much trust to place in them. The role of Explainable AI (XAI) in code review and its impact on trust remain underexplored.
  Objective: We study the influence of XAI on developer trust in AI-assisted code reviews.
  Method: We conducted a within-subjects user study with 34 participants, comparing three LLM-based code review systems with varying levels of XAI support: Condition A (detailed explanation and review feedback), Condition B (review feedback only), and Condition C (no explanations). Participants reviewed real-world code change requests alongside the AI-generated reviews. We measured trust perceptions, agreement with the AI recommendation, the reasoning given for each decision, and the time taken.
  Results: The level of explanation significantly influences both trust and agreement with AI recommendations, but in different ways. Full explanations (A) yield the highest perceived trust (M = 3.99/5) but not the highest agreement, whereas moderate explanations (B) achieve the highest agreement (89.22%). This could suggest that more explanation prompts developers to question AI recommendations more frequently. No explanations (C) results in the lowest trust and agreement. Explanation level did not significantly affect review time. The most commonly cited reasons for decisions were code readability and correctness.
  Conclusion: Incorporating XAI into code review significantly changes trust perceptions and agreement with AI recommendations. These results inform the design and evaluation of trustworthy AI-based code review systems, as well as studies on the human factors of AI-assisted software development.

</details>


### [25] [Verification-Conditioned Use: A Qualitative Study on How Generative AI Reshapes Learning, Autonomy, and Market Entry for Junior Software Developers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24606)
*Pedro Henrique Andriotte,Danilo Monteiro Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.SE

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 本研究通过对13名实习生和初级开发者的访谈，发现AI使用可持续的关键在于验证条件性使用，而非工具本身；新手面临自主性悖论和形成性悖论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究生成式AI如何影响新手软件开发者职业早期阶段，特别是学习、自主性和市场适应等方面。

Method: 对13名实习生和初级开发者进行视频会议访谈，采用Braun和Clarke主题分析六阶段法进行数据分析。

Result: 识别出16个主题，核心是验证条件性使用；发现自主性悖论和第一人称否认依赖；提出形成性悖论，即AI导致的浅层学习阻碍批判性判断能力的发展。

Conclusion: AI使用的可持续性依赖于个人实践：审查后接受、拒绝盲目使用、寻求解释以及保持刻意练习。

中文摘要：目的：研究生成式人工智能工具的使用如何影响软件开发者职业生涯的早期阶段，从新手自身的视角出发。方法：通过视频会议单独采访了十三名实习生和初级开发者。采用Braun和Clarke的主题分析六阶段法，进行归纳编码和语义分析。结果：围绕一个核心概念——验证条件性使用——产生了十六个主题。在研究的四个研究问题（使用模式、学习、自主性和市场进入）中，决定AI与人工工作的最常见标准不是截止日期或任务复杂性，而是检查结果的能力。两个主题揭示了新手自我认知中的紧张关系：自主性悖论（感觉更有能力但对结果的所有权更少）和第一人称否认依赖。这些发现共同指向一个理论贡献——形成性悖论：AI引发的浅层学习使得建立参与者认为市场开始要求的批判性判断能力变得更加困难。结论：从参与者自身的角度来看，使AI使用可持续的不是工具本身，而是在接受前审查、拒绝在不理解的情况下使用AI、向工具寻求解释以及在AI辅助工作之外保持刻意练习的个人实践。

Original abstract: Objective: to investigate how the use of generative Artificial Intelligence (AI) tools affects the early stages of a career in software development, from the perspective of the newcomers themselves. Method: thirteen interns and junior developers were interviewed individually, by videoconference. Interviews were analyzed using the six phases of Braun and Clarke's thematic analysis, with inductive coding and a semantic approach. Results: sixteen themes emerged, organized around a central concept: verification-conditioned use. Across the study's four research questions (usage patterns, learning, autonomy, and market entry), the criterion that most often decides between AI and manual work is not deadline or task complexity, but the ability to check the result. Two themes expose tensions in newcomers' self-perception: the autonomy paradox (feeling more capable yet less in ownership of the result) and the first-person denial of dependence. Together, these findings point to a theoretical contribution, the formative paradox: the shallow learning that AI induces makes it harder to build the very critical-judgment competence that, according to participants, the market has begun to demand. Conclusion: what makes AI use sustainable, from participants' own point of view, is not the tool itself but the individual practice of reviewing before accepting, refusing to use AI without understanding it, asking the tool for explanations, and keeping deliberate practice outside of AI-assisted work.

</details>

### [26] [DeepLens Diagnosis Agent: Agentic Workflow Design Lets a Small Reasoning Model Compete with Frontier LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22555)
*Mahmood Bayeshi,Veysel Kocaman,Muhammed Ali Naqvi,Yigit Gul,David Talby*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 20 pages, 5 figures, 5 tables. Technical report, John Snow Labs

Selection reason: 提出了可迁移的agentic workflow（五阶段管道），结合检索和约束生成，通用性强。

TL;DR: 提出一个五阶段诊断代理流水线，结合小模型与RAG，在低成本下显著提升诊断准确率，并支持可审计的中间结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿LLM在单次提示下诊断推理脆弱，且知识回忆不足以应对不确定性，需要结构化流程约束来提升可靠性和可解释性。

Method: DeepLens诊断代理，以JSL Medical Small 7B v2和RAG为核心，包含临床提取、检索、候选生成、证据三角验证和最终决策五个阶段。

Result: 在DiagnosisArena上top-1准确率60.14%，相比无工作流提升36个百分点，成本低于Claude/Gemini且性能更优，延迟24秒。

Conclusion: 结构化工作流能显著提升小模型诊断性能，提供可审计的中间产物，适用于高可靠性场景，且成本效益优于大模型。

中文摘要：医学诊断是一个多阶段过程：提取事实、咨询知识、生成鉴别分析，并选择最佳诊断同时给出解释。前沿大语言模型是强大的通才，但单次提示通常导致脆弱的诊断推理。我们提出了DeepLens诊断代理，一个五阶段驾驭流水线（将模型能力与严格的流程约束相结合），以一个小型医学推理模型（JSL Medical Small 7B v2）和检索增强生成（RAG）为核心。该流水线强制执行结构化的临床提取、严格的检索、受限的候选生成、明确的证据三角验证以及可审计的最终决策。在包含915个病例的DiagnosisArena基准测试上，该代理达到了60.14%的top-1诊断准确率，是小型和中等规模模型中最高。同一模型在没有代理工作流的情况下仅达到23.99%，仅工作流设计就带来了+36个百分点的提升，尽管该模型在标准医学基准上达到88.2%，这表明不确定性下的诊断推理需要的不仅仅是知识回忆。该代理每次病例成本为0.0072美元（A100上24K tokens），延迟24秒，比Claude Sonnet 4.5（0.0110美元）和Gemini 3.1 Pro（0.0128美元）便宜35-45%，同时性能分别高出9.70个百分点和9.17个百分点。驾驭还可以纠正前沿模型的失败；工作流约束可以超过参数数量或API成本。除了总体准确率，该流水线还生成结构化的中间产物，使每个阶段都可检查并支持错误定位。这些特性支持高风险场景，其中可追溯性、可重复性和可审计证据与基准性能同样重要。

Original abstract: Medical diagnosis is a multi-stage process: extract facts, consult knowledge, generate a differential analysis, and select the best diagnosis with explanations. Frontier LLMs are strong generalists, but single-shot prompting often yields brittle diagnostic reasoning. We present the DeepLens Diagnosis Agent, a five-stage harnessing pipeline (combining model capabilities with disciplined process constraints) centered on a small medical reasoning model (JSL Medical Small 7B v2) and retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). The pipeline enforces structured clinical extraction, disciplined retrieval, constrained candidate generation, explicit evidence triangulation, and an auditable final decision. On the 915-case DiagnosisArena benchmark, the agent achieved 60.14% top-1 diagnostic accuracy, the highest among small and medium-sized models. The same model without the agent workflow achieved 23.99%, a +36-point gain from workflow design alone, despite 88.2% on standard medical benchmarks, showing that diagnostic reasoning under uncertainty requires more than knowledge recall. The agent costs USD 0.0072 per case (24K tokens on A100) with 24-second latency, 35-45% cheaper than Claude Sonnet 4.5 (USD 0.0110) and Gemini 3.1 Pro (USD 0.0128) while outperforming them by +9.70pp and +9.17pp. Harnessing can also correct frontier model failures; workflow constraints can outweigh parameter count or API cost.
  Beyond aggregate accuracy, the pipeline produces structured intermediate artifacts that make each stage inspectable and support error localization. These properties support high-stakes settings where traceability, reproducibility, and auditable evidence matter alongside benchmark performance.

</details>


### [27] [Codifying the Judge: Scalable Evaluation via Program Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22561)
*Tzu-Heng Huang,Shengqi Qiu,Frederic Sala*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 程序蒸馏评估方法，可迁移到agent自动评估与验证。

TL;DR: 提出程序蒸馏和PAJAMA系统，用程序化评判者替代LLM作为评判者，在降低成本的同时保持性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为评判者成本高、延迟大、决策不透明，影响可扩展性和可靠性。

Method: 程序蒸馏：将LLM决策逻辑蒸馏到程序委员会中；PAJAMA系统：合成程序评判者、聚合决策、低置信度回退至LLM。

Result: 程序化评判者匹配13B LLM性能；PAJAMA提升准确率和吞吐量；奖励模型在RewardBench上优于专有LLM标签训练的模型，成本低两个数量级。

Conclusion: 程序化评判者是高效、透明、低成本的替代方案，在评估和奖励信号方面均有效。

中文摘要：LLM作为评判者已成为自动化评估的标准，但存在高昂成本、显著延迟和决策不透明等问题——这些局限性削弱了其可扩展性和可靠性。我们提出一种简单高效的替代方案：程序蒸馏。不在评估时提示LLM，而是将其决策逻辑蒸馏到一个由多个程序组成的委员会中，直接对候选结果进行评分。这些程序化评判者具有透明性，易于检查或编辑，并消除了每次样本的API成本。基于这一概念，我们引入了PAJAMA系统，该系统将程序合成为评判者，聚合它们的决策形成联合裁决，并包含一个回退机制，可选择性地将低置信度案例升级到LLM。在五个数据集和四个模型家族上，我们展示了程序化评判者能够匹配13B规模LLM评判者的性能。当使用程序输出作为路由信号时，PAJAMA提高了准确性和吞吐量，并推进了帕累托前沿。在评估之外，程序化评判者还能产生廉价且有效的奖励信号：在RewardBench上，从程序裁决中蒸馏出的奖励模型，其性能优于在专有LLM标签上训练的模型，而API成本低两个数量级。

Original abstract: LLM-as-a-judge has become the standard for automated evaluation, but it suffers from high cost, significant latency, and opaque decisions -- limitations that undermine its scalability and reliability. We address these with a simple, efficient alternative: program distillation. Instead of prompting an LLM at the evaluation time, we distill its decision logic into a committee of programs that score candidates directly. These programmatic judges offer transparency, are easily inspected or edited, and eliminate per-sample API costs. Building on this notion, we introduce PAJAMA, a system that synthesizes programs as judges, aggregates their decisions into a joint verdict, and incorporates a fallback mechanism to selectively escalate low-confidence cases to an LLM. Across five datasets and four model families, we show that programmatic judges can match the performance of a 13B-size LLM judge. When using program outputs as routing signals, PAJAMA improves both accuracy and throughput and advances the Pareto frontier. Beyond evaluation, programmatic judges produce cheap and effective reward signals: on RewardBench, a reward model distilled from programs' verdicts outperforms one trained on a proprietary LLM's labels at two orders of magnitude lower API cost.

</details>


### [28] [SF-AMS: Strategic Forgetting for Structured Memory in LLM Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22562)
*Ning Yang,Siqi Li,Miaoxin Shen,Yuan Zhou,Meng Zhang,Tong Li,Haijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 直接提出LLM agent记忆管理框架，对coding agent长程任务关键。

TL;DR: 提出SF-AMS框架，通过效用驱动的记忆重要性建模和复合重要性评分，在长上下文推理中取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 管理长上下文依赖是LLM代理的主要瓶颈，冗余和无关信息降低多步推理性能。

Method: 提出SF-AMS，用效用驱动的存续机制替代静态检索，结合使用冗余和时序信号更新记忆重要性，并引入复合重要性评分整合语义和实体信号。

Result: 在LoCoMo和LongMemEval-s上，SF-AMS在多种任务和骨干网络下超越强基线，多跳推理提升+9.65 F1，时序推理+6.91 F1，开放域+6.53 F1。

Conclusion: 将记忆重要性建模为动态效用信号对于可靠的长上下文推理至关重要。

中文摘要：管理长上下文依赖仍然是LLM代理的主要瓶颈，因为冗余和无关的信息会降低多步推理的性能。提出了用于代理记忆系统的策略性遗忘（SF-AMS）框架，通过建模记忆单元的长期重要性来维持紧凑的高效用记忆。SF-AMS用效用驱动的存续机制替代了静态检索和启发式衰减，该机制根据使用冗余和时序信号更新记忆重要性，从而产生一种层级记忆结构，优先保留稳定的、实体一致的信息，同时过滤噪声。在此基础上，复合重要性评分整合了语义和实体级别的信号以提高检索鲁棒性。在LoCoMo和LongMemEval-s上的实验表明，相较于包括LightMem、MemO和A-Mem在内的强基准方法，SF-AMS取得了持续的性能提升。最大的改进出现在Qwen2.5-7B下的多跳推理中，SF-AMS相对于最强基线提升了+9.65 F1，其次是GPT-4o-mini下的时序推理提升了+6.91 F1，以及开放域任务提升了+6.53 F1，展示了强大的跨骨干网络泛化能力。这些结果表明，将记忆重要性建模为动态效用信号对于可靠的长上下文推理至关重要。

Original abstract: Managing long-context dependencies remains a primary bottleneck in LLM agents, as redundant and irrelevant information can degrade multi-step reasoning. Strategic Forgetting for Agent Memory Systems (SF-AMS) is proposed as a framework for maintaining compact high-utility memory by modeling the long-term importance of memory units. SF-AMS replaces static retrieval and heuristic decay with a utility-driven survival mechanism that updates memory importance from usage redundancy and temporal signals, inducing a hierarchical memory structure that prioritizes stable entity-consistent information while filtering noise. On top of this, Composite Importance Scoring integrates semantic and entity level signals to improve retrieval robustness. Experiments on LoCoMo and LongMemEval-s show consistent gains over strong state of the art baselines including LightMem MemO and A-Mem.
