The largest improvement appears in multi-hop reasoning under Qwen2.5-7B where SF-AMS achieves plus 9.65 F1 over the strongest baseline followed by temporal reasoning under GPT-4o-mini plus 6.91 F1 and open-domain tasks plus 6.53 F1 demonstrating strong cross backbone generalization. These results show that modeling memory importance as a dynamic utility signal is critical for reliable long-context reasoning.

</details>


### [29] [Synthetic Scenario Generation for Evaluation of Industry 4.0 Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22563)
*Sagar Chethan Kumar,Rohith Kanathur,Dhaval Patel,Kaoutar El Maghraoui*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 19 pages, 3 appendices

Selection reason: 工业场景的合成数据生成agent，方法可迁移到代码agent基准测试。

TL;DR: 本文通过扩展AssetOpsBench并引入ScenarioGeneratorAgent流水线，实现了基于标准的合成场景生成，优化后速度提升8倍且质量相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工业智能体基准测试依赖手动编写场景且资产类别有限，需要更高效、可扩展的场景生成方法。

Method: 提出ScenarioGeneratorAgent流水线，包含证据驱动的资产配置、覆盖感知预算分配、混合验证-修复循环，以及缓存、并行化等优化。

Result: 在智能电网变压器场景生成中，优化后端到端速度提升8倍，综合质量得分74.2±1.9（基线73.8±3.0）。

Conclusion: 基于标准的合成场景生成能高效扩展工业基准测试，且不牺牲质量。

中文摘要：工业智能体基准测试需要结合遥测、故障模式、维护记录和领域标准的逼真评估场景。然而，现有的基准测试（如AssetOpsBench）依赖于手动编写的场景，且覆盖的资产类别有限。我们为AssetOpsBench扩展了一个智能电网变压器资产类别和四个基于IEC的诊断工具，用于健康指数预测、溶解气体分析、绕组温度评估和负载曲线评估。我们进一步引入了ScenarioGeneratorAgent，这是一个用于合成工业智能体场景生成的流水线。该流水线构建基于证据的资产配置文件，跨运营域分配覆盖感知的场景预算，并通过混合验证-修复循环生成候选场景，该循环强制执行模式有效性、工具可达性、物理合理性、标准一致性和去重。为了提高可扩展性，我们应用了两级缓存、并行焦点组生成、线程池卸载、批量LLM调用和早期拒绝过滤。在智能电网变压器场景生成中，这些优化将50个场景的端到端运行时减少了8倍，同时保持了质量，实现了74.2±1.9的综合质量得分，而未经优化的基线为73.8±3.0。这些结果表明，基于标准的合成场景生成可以在不牺牲场景质量的情况下有效扩展工业智能体基准测试。

Original abstract: Industrial agent benchmarks require realistic evaluation scenarios that integrate telemetry, failure modes, maintenance records, and domain standards. However, existing benchmarks such as AssetOpsBench rely on manually authored scenarios and cover a limited set of asset classes. We extend AssetOpsBench with a Smart Grid Transformer asset class and four IEC-grounded diagnostic tools for health-index prediction, dissolved-gas analysis, winding-temperature assessment, and load-profile assessment. We further introduce ScenarioGeneratorAgent, a pipeline for synthetic industrial-agent scenario generation. The pipeline constructs evidence-grounded asset profiles, allocates coverage-aware scenario budgets across operational domains, and generates candidates through a hybrid validation-and-repair loop that enforces schema validity, tool reachability, physical plausibility, standards alignment, and deduplication. To improve scalability, we apply two-level caching, parallel focus-group generation, thread-pool offloading, batched LLM calls, and early rejection filtering. On Smart Grid Transformer scenario generation, these optimizations reduce end-to-end runtime by $8\times$ for 50 scenarios while preserving quality, achieving a composite quality score of $74.2 \pm 1.9$ compared with $73.8 \pm 3.0$ for the unoptimized baseline. These results show that standards-grounded synthetic scenario generation can efficiently expand industrial-agent benchmarks without sacrificing scenario quality.

</details>


### [30] [Execution-Grounded Security Testing for Coding Agents in Software Engineering Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22569)
*Yifei Ge,Weisong Sun,Jinkun Xiao,Yuchen Chen,Yebo Feng,Peizhuo Lv,Xia Feng,Chunrong Fang,Zhihong Zhao,Zhenyu Chen,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Preprint. 12 pages, 6 figures

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 提出执行基础的红色团队测试框架，将不安全操作嵌入常规任务，检测编码代理在系统操作中的执行层安全漏洞，实验显示代码载体不安全执行率达73.61%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码代理在系统操作中可直接修改环境，其工具调用可能触发持久性恶意更改，现有安全测试忽视执行层行为，需要探测代理实际对系统的操作。

Method: 构建执行基础的红色团队测试框架，将目标不安全操作伪装成单元测试、回归测试等常规任务，利用沙盒证据（工具调用、运行时追踪、文件系统差异）分析，并通过执行预言机迭代优化探测。

Result: 在多个代理框架上，代码载体不安全执行率达73.61%，文本载体达53.93%，表明伪装任务可诱导代理执行不安全操作。

Conclusion: 编码代理在系统操作中仍不安全，任务伪装能绕过安全检测，需要更强的安全测试和防护机制。

中文摘要：编码代理越来越多地集成到系统操作中，它们的工具使用可以直接修改项目工件、执行环境和底层系统。例如，如果编码代理在系统启动或配置脚本中插入一个钩子，该更改可以在交互后持续存在，稍后被触发，并滥用委托的用户或系统权限来修改系统。这使得安全测试成为一个系统问题：关键问题不仅是代理说了什么，而是它实际对周围环境做了什么。我们提出了一个执行基础的红色团队测试框架，用于使用可观察的沙盒证据（包括工具调用、运行时跟踪和文件系统差异）来探测这个执行层安全边界。我们的框架将目标不安全操作嵌入到常规软件工程工作负载中，包括单元测试、回归测试、崩溃重现和验证，并使用执行预言机指导初始探测被拒绝或失败时的改进。在多个代理框架和模型骨干上，我们的红色团队工作负载重新公式化显著增加了已验证的不安全执行，代码载体达到73.61%，文本载体达到53.93%。这些结果表明，系统操作中的编码代理在任务伪装下仍然不安全：一旦风险意图隐藏在看似合理的工程任务中，代理可能被诱导对周围系统执行不安全操作。更广泛地说，系统操作中的编码代理仍然需要更强的安全测试和防护措施。

Original abstract: Coding agents are increasingly integrated into system operations, where their tool use can directly modify project artifacts, execution environments, and the underlying system. For example, if a coding agent inserts a hook into a system startup or configuration script, that change can persist after the interaction, be triggered later, and abuse delegated user or system privileges to modify the system. This makes security testing a system problem: the key question is not only what the agent says, but what it actually does to the surrounding environment. We present an execution-grounded red-team testing framework for probing this execution-layer security boundary using observable sandbox evidence, including tool invocations, runtime traces, and file-system diffs. Our framework embeds target unsafe operations into routine software engineering workloads, including unit testing, regression testing, crash reproduction, and validation, and uses an execution oracle to guide refinement when an initial probe is rejected or fails. Across multiple agent frameworks and model backbones, our red-team workload reformulation substantially increases verified unsafe execution, reaching 73.61% on code carriers and 53.93% on text carriers. These results show that coding agents in system operations remain insecure under task disguise: once risky intent is hidden inside plausible engineering tasks, the agent can be induced to carry out unsafe actions on the surrounding system. More broadly, coding agents in system operations still demand stronger security testing and safeguards.

</details>


### [31] [SCAIR: Schema-Conditioned Agentic Iterative Reasoning for Enterprise Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22571)
*Prateek Chaturvedi,Yuqicheng Zhu,Hongkuan Zhou,Dongzhuoran Zhou,Yunjie He,Steffen Staab,Fei Du,Jie Tang,Evgeny Kharlamov*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Accepted at ACL 2026 Industry Track

Selection reason: 训练自由的agentic KG-RAG框架，规划和迭代推理可迁移到代码优化。

TL;DR: SCAIR通过注入模式条件结构先验和模式感知遍历，显著提升了企业知识图谱上的检索增强生成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KG-RAG方法在公共基准上表现良好，但无法泛化到真实企业知识图谱（密集、模式驱动、受操作约束），需要新的方法。

Method: 提出SCAIR，一种无训练框架，通过注入模式条件的结构先验和强制执行模式感知遍历，实现结构化规划与受控迭代推理的结合。

Result: 在基于真实CMDB的企业基准上，SCAIR显著优于现有KG-RAG方法。

Conclusion: 可靠的企业图推理必须显式纳入目标领域的结构和操作约束，通过使智能体设计与业务逻辑对齐，可以在无需模型重训下取得显著性能提升。

中文摘要：基于知识图谱的检索增强生成（KG-RAG）实现了与结构化企业知识的自然语言交互，然而现有的在公共基准测试中表现良好的基于智能体的方法通常无法泛化到真实的企业知识图谱（KG），这些图谱通常密集、由模式驱动且受操作约束。为了解决这些局限性，我们提出SCAIR（模式条件智能体迭代推理），这是一种无训练框架，通过注入模式条件的结构先验并在多跳推理过程中强制执行模式感知遍历，将结构化规划与受控迭代推理相结合。在从真实世界配置管理数据库（CMDB）构建的企业导向基准上的实验表明，SCAIR显著优于现有的KG-RAG方法。至关重要的是，我们的研究强调，可靠的企业图推理不能依赖通用的智能体设计，而必须明确地将目标领域的结构和操作约束纳入推理过程。我们证明，通过使智能体设计与业务逻辑对齐，可以在不需要昂贵模型重新训练的情况下实现显著的性能提升。

Original abstract: Knowledge Graph-based Retrieval-Augmented Generation (KG-RAG) enables natural language interaction with structured enterprise knowledge, yet existing agentic approaches that perform well on public benchmarks often fail to generalize to real-world enterprise Knowledge Graphs (KGs), which are dense, schema-driven, and operationally constrained. To address these limitations, we propose SCAIR (Schema-Conditioned Agentic Iterative Reasoning), a training-free framework that integrates structured planning with controlled iterative reasoning by injecting schema-conditioned structural priors and enforcing schema-aware traversal during multi-hop reasoning. Experiments on an enterprise-oriented benchmark constructed from a real-world Configuration Management DataBase (CMDB) demonstrate that SCAIR substantially improves performance over existing KG-RAG methods. Crucially, our study highlights that reliable enterprise graph reasoning cannot rely on generic agentic designs; instead, it must explicitly incorporate the target domain's structural and operational constraints into the reasoning process. We demonstrate that by aligning agent design with business logic, substantial performance gains can be achieved without the need for costly model retraining.

</details>


### [32] [Schema-Aware Localisation (SAL): Live Schema Grounding and Hallucination Validation for Oracle NL2SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22572)
*Sanjay Mishra,Divya Chukkapalli,Ganesh R. Naik*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 18 pages , 3 figures , 14 tables ,1 algorithm

Selection reason: 实时schema grounding与幻觉验证，可用于coding agent的SQL生成。

TL;DR: 提出SAL方法，通过实时模式映射和幻觉索引检查，显著提升Oracle NL2SQL的执行准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在Oracle NL2SQL中因缺乏模式接地而导致大量执行失败（如ORA-00904错误），需要一种无需重新训练就能提供准确模式信息的方案。

Method: SAL（Schema-Aware Localisation）查询Oracle的USER_TAB_COLUMNS目录构建实时模式映射，为每个问题选择相关表子集并注入LLM提示；随后通过Hallucination Index（Hidx）验证SQL中的别名.列引用，自动修正前缀错误或触发结构化重试。

Result: 在500个TPC-H问题上，无模式接地时EGT为2.2%，SAL达到62.6% EGT（简单96%，中等95%，复杂40.7%），执行失败率从97.6%降至2.6%。

Conclusion: SAL无需手动模式整理或模型重训练，即可大幅提升Oracle NL2SQL的执行准确率，是一种轻量级且有效的解决方案。

中文摘要：大语言模型可以从自然语言生成流畅的SQL，但在真实的企业级Oracle数据库上，它们经常在执行时失败：列名和别名被幻觉，方言特定的语法被遗漏，导致ORA-00904无效标识符错误。在这种情况下，失败主要是由于缺少模式接地：模型无法知道哪些表和列实际存在。本文介绍了模式感知定位（SAL），这是一种用于Oracle NL2SQL的轻量级中间件层，无需重新训练模型。SAL查询Oracle的USER_TAB_COLUMNS目录以构建实时模式映射，为每个问题选择相关的表子集（对于多表查询回退到完整模式），并将这些真实上下文注入到LLM提示中。生成的SQL然后由幻觉索引（Hidx）检查，该索引根据实时目录验证每个别名.列引用，自动重写可预测的前缀错误，否则触发带有逐项修正的结构化重试。我们使用GPT-4o-mini在针对实时Oracle Autonomous Database 23c实例执行的500个TPC-H自然语言问题上评估了SAL。没有任何模式接地时，执行接地真值（EGT；执行并匹配参考结果集）为2.2%（12/500）。手写的静态模式提示将EGT提高到62.0%。SAL无需手动模式整理，实现了62.6%的EGT（简单查询96%，中等查询95%，复杂查询40.7%），同时将执行失败从97.6%降低到2.6%。

Original abstract: Large language models can generate fluent SQL from natural language, but on real enterprise Oracle databases they frequently fail at execution time: columns and aliases are hallucinated and dialect-specific syntax is missed, leading to ORA-00904 invalid-identifier errors. In this setting, failures are primarily due to missing schema grounding: the model cannot know which tables and columns actually exist. This paper introduces Schema-Aware Localisation (SAL), a lightweight middleware layer for Oracle NL2SQL that requires no model retraining. SAL queries Oracle's USER_TAB_COLUMNS catalog to build a live schema map, selects a relevant table subset for each question (falling back to the full schema for multi-table queries), and injects this ground-truth context into the LLM prompt. Generated SQL is then checked by the Hallucination Index (Hidx), which validates every alias.column reference against the live catalog, automatically rewrites predictable prefix errors, and otherwise triggers a structured retry with itemised corrections. We evaluate SAL on 500 TPC-H natural language questions executed against a live Oracle Autonomous Database 23c instance using GPT-4o-mini. Without any schema grounding, execution-grounded truth (EGT; executes and matches the reference result set) is 2.2% (12/500). A hand-written static schema hint brings EGT to 62.0%. SAL, with no manual schema curation, achieves 62.6% EGT (96% simple, 95% medium, 40.7% complex) while reducing execution failures from 97.6% to 2.6%.

</details>


### [33] [Too much evidence, too little time: From text to actionable recommendations through multi-objective evidence reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22574)
*Adela Bara,Simona-Vasilica Oprea*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 多目标证据推理框架，可迁移到agent规划与决策。

TL;DR: SCEPTER是一个框架，通过结合检索、排序、声明提取、矛盾检测和帕累托选择，将临床病例描述转化为证据建议，在150个案例中实现了192:1的压缩比和高多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在时间限制下，临床专家难以手动审查PubMed返回的数百篇文献，因此需要自动化的文献综合和推荐系统。

Method: SCEPTER结合PubMed检索、PubMedBERT语义排序、LLM声明提取、证据加权、矛盾检测、共识分析和多目标帕累托声明选择，生成结构化证据和推荐。

Result: 在150个案例中，搜索空间从平均576篇论文减少到53篇、7个帕累托最优声明和3个推荐，压缩比192:1，证据多样性熵为0.901。

Conclusion: 多目标帕累托选择在保持证据多样性的同时有效压缩文献，优于传统排序方法，为临床决策提供自动化的证据综合支持。

中文摘要：基于证据的临床决策要求专家识别、评估和综合相关科学文献。然而，针对复杂临床病例的PubMed搜索通常会返回数百篇出版物，在时间限制下无法手动审查。本研究提出SCEPTER（单病例证据驱动的PubMed到推荐推理器），一种将临床病例描述转化为基于证据的建议的框架。SCEPTER结合了PubMed检索、PubMedBERT语义排序、基于大语言模型（LLM）的声明提取、证据级别加权、矛盾检测、共识分析和多目标帕累托声明选择。该框架生成结构化的证据综合和可操作的合理建议。一个论文问答模块进一步支持对所选出版物的交互式探索。所提出的框架引入了一个多目标推理模型，将文献支持、矛盾分析和交互式文献查询整合到统一的临床决策支持管道中。在150个案例研究上的评估表明，该框架将平均576篇论文的搜索空间减少到53篇保留论文、7个帕累托最优声明和3个最终建议，总体压缩比为192:1。尽管有这种减少，保留的证据保持了高多样性（熵=0.901）。消融研究表明，与传统的排序方法相比，基于帕累托的选择提高了证据多样性和建议效用。

Original abstract: Evidence-based clinical decision making requires specialists to identify, evaluate and synthesize relevant scientific literature. However, PubMed searches for complex clinical cases often return hundreds of publications that cannot be reviewed manually under time constraints. This study proposes SCEPTER (Single-Case Evidence-driven PubMed-To-rEcommendation Reasoner), a framework for transforming clinical case descriptions into evidence-based recommendations. SCEPTER combines PubMed retrieval, PubMedBERT semantic ranking, large language model (LLM)-based claim extraction, evidence-level weighting, contradiction detection, consensus analysis and multi-objective Pareto claim selection. The framework generates structured evidence syntheses and grounded actionable recommendations. A Paper Q&A module further enables interactive exploration of selected publications. The proposed framework introduces multi-objective reasoning model that integrates literature support, contradiction analysis and interactive literature interrogation into a unified clinical decision-support pipeline. Evaluation on 150 case studies demonstrated that the framework reduced an average search space of 576 papers to 53 retained papers, 7 Pareto-optimal claims and 3 final recommendations, corresponding to an overall compression ratio of 192:1. Despite this reduction, the retained evidence maintained high diversity (entropy=0.901). The ablation study showed that Pareto-based selection increased evidence diversity and recommendation utility compared with conventional ranking approaches.

</details>


### [34] [CRAFT: Learn the Schema, Execute the Plan](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22642)
*Aakash Kolekar,Sahika Genc,Shahriar Shariat,Bunyamin Sisman,Tibor Mezi,Barbara Poblete,Shree Vandana Kachroo,Calvin Chi,Parth Parmar,Ari Singer,Prayaas Jain,Cindy Barker,Benoit Dumoulin*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: CRAFT是一种两阶段后训练方法，通过无模式监督微调和执行形状强化学习，使编码代理获取稳定的模式知识和工具使用行为，在广告分析任务中相比基线提升性能并降低推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的企业编码代理通过在每个提示中注入详尽的模式和工具文档，导致推理开销高、模式演化困难和多轮分析可靠性差。需要一种方法让代理通过后训练获取稳定的知识，同时保持一致性。

Method: 提出CRAFT，包含两个阶段：1）无模式PLAN监督微调，从验证轨迹中学习领域结构和行为；2）执行形状强化学习，优化工具选择、代码质量等。使用三闸滤波器（执行验证、数据完整性检查、LLM审计）筛选训练数据。

Result: 在广告分析评估中，CRAFT相比填充模式基线，Agent Score提高+9.6pp，一致性+4.1pp，多轮连贯性+4.2pp，输入令牌减少约9倍，模式发现循环减少5倍。

Conclusion: CRAFT通过后训练有效提升了企业编码代理的性能和效率，同时讨论了部署权衡和训练扩展需求，为多轮工具使用强化学习在企业场景中的应用提供了探索。

中文摘要：企业编码代理将自然语言分析请求转换为可执行代码，这些代码在专有API、模式和指标定义上运行。然而，当前主流的部署模式将详尽的模式和工具文档注入到每个提示中，这增加了推理开销，使模式演化复杂化，并削弱了多轮分析的可靠性。我们研究是否可以通过后训练获取稳定的模式知识和工具使用行为，同时保持生产环境分析所需的一致性。我们提出CRAFT，一种用于模式接地的编码代理的两阶段后训练方案。首先，无模式PLAN监督微调从经过验证的轨迹中学习领域结构化计划和可执行行为，而无需在提示时注入详尽的模式。其次，执行形状的强化学习调整策略以优化工具选择、代码质量、计划-代码一致性以及从失败执行中恢复。训练轨迹通过三门滤波器进行筛选，该滤波器结合了执行验证、数据完整性检查和LLM判断推理审计。我们在广告分析的计划推广中评估CRAFT，涵盖广告系列表现分析、指标下钻、实体级表现分析和多轮分析优化。企业评估环境包含作为代理面向工具表面的测试版API，并涵盖25个模式链接的核心实体和30个代理工作流。相对于填充模式的基线，CRAFT将复合Agent Score提高了+9.6个百分点，一致性提高了+4.1个百分点，多轮连贯性提高了+4.2个百分点，同时将输入令牌负担降低了约9倍，模式发现循环减少了多达5倍。我们进一步报告了部署权衡、奖励塑造局限性以及企业环境中多轮工具使用强化学习所需的训练基础设施扩展。

Original abstract: Enterprise coding agents translate natural-language analytical requests into executable code over proprietary APIs, schemas, and metric definitions. Yet the prevailing deployment pattern injecting exhaustive schema and tool documentation into each prompt increases inference overhead, complicates schema evolution, and undermines reliability in multi-turn analysis. We investigate whether stable schema knowledge and tool-use behavior can instead be acquired through post-training while preserving the consistency required for production-facing analytics. We present CRAFT, a two-stage post-training recipe for schema-grounded coding agents. First, schema-stripped PLAN supervised fine-tuning learns domain-structured plans and executable behaviors from validated trajectories without exhaustive prompt-time schema injection. Second, execution-shaped reinforcement learning aligns the policy for tool selection, code quality, plan-code consistency, and recovery from failed executions. Training trajectories are curated through a Tri-Gate filter combining execution validation, data-integrity checks, and LLM-judge reasoning audit. We evaluate CRAFT for planned rollout in advertising analytics, covering campaign performance analysis, metric drill-downs, entity-level performance analysis, and multi-turn analytical refinement. The enterprise evaluation environment incorporates beta APIs as the agent-facing tool surface and spans 25 schema-linked core entities and 30 agentic workflows. Relative to a schema-stuffed baseline, CRAFT improves composite Agent Score by +9.6 pp, consistency by +4.1 pp, and multi-turn coherence by +4.2 pp, while reducing input-token burden by approximately 9x and schema-discovery loops by up to 5x. We further report deployment tradeoffs, reward-shaping limitations, and training-infrastructure extensions required for multi-turn tool-use reinforcement learning in enterprise settings.

</details>


### [35] [How Well Can AI Generate Backlogs from App Mockups?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22902)
*Andrea Lezcano Airaldi,Lourdes Romera,Walid Maalej*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Accepted at the AIRE Workshop, IEEE 34th International Requirements Engineering Conference (RE) 2026

Selection reason: cs.SE 论文按全量策略收录。

TL;DR: 提出多模态方法从视觉模型生成冲刺待办事项，评估三种提示策略，发现混合提示加架构上下文效果较好，并提出修正召回率度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 创建冲刺待办事项列表费力且易出错，需要自动生成方法。

Method: 使用GPT-4o评估零样本、CCoT和角色驱动三种提示策略，并引入架构上下文；提出修正召回率度量。

Result: 基线偏好召回，CCoT更平衡（F1 52-66%）；任务生成困难；后端任务精度提升35%；26%误报被认为有用。

Conclusion: 混合提示加架构上下文可辅助生成，但结果因条目类型而异，仍需人工监督。

中文摘要：创建冲刺待办事项列表需要相当大的努力，因为诸如史诗、用户故事和任务等条目可能会被遗漏或规格不一致。我们提出了一种多模态方法来支持从视觉应用程序模型（在项目早期阶段可用的工件）生成待办事项列表。我们评估了GPT-4o上的三种提示策略：零样本基线、用于视觉语言推理的组合思维链（CCoT）和角色驱动提示。我们研究了跨两个国家的七个应用程序开发项目，并就结果采访了开发人员。总体而言，我们观察到基线提示偏好召回率而非精确率，而CCoT更加平衡，在史诗和用户故事方面实现了52-66%的平均F1分数。任务更难准确生成。添加架构上下文时，精确率提升最为一致，特别是对于后端任务（精确率提升高达35%）。对开发人员的采访显示，高达26%的误报仍被认为有用，这反映了待办事项列表创建的创造性和开放性。为了捕捉这一点，我们提出了一种称为修正召回率的新度量，该度量用开发人员评估补充了真实值评估。我们的发现表明，带有架构上下文的混合提示可以辅助从早期模型生成待办事项列表，但结果因条目类型而异，并且开发人员的监督仍然是必要的。

Original abstract: Creating sprint backlogs requires considerable effort, as items such as epics, user stories, and tasks can be missed or inconsistently specified. We propose a multimodal approach to support backlog generation from visual app mockups, an artifact available at early project stages. We evaluate three prompting strategies on GPT-4o: a zero-shot baseline, Compositional Chain-of-Thought (CCoT) for vision-language reasoning, and a persona-driven prompt. We study seven app development projects across two countries and interview developers about the results. Overall, we observed that the baseline prompt favours recall over precision, whereas CCoT is more balanced, achieving average F1 scores of 52-66% for epics and user stories. Tasks were more challenging to generate accurately. Precision gains were most consistent when adding architectural context, particularly for backend tasks (precision gains up to 35%). Interviews with developers revealed that up to 26% of false positives were still considered useful, reflecting the creative and open-ended nature of backlog creation. To capture this, we propose a new measure called Revised Recall, which complements ground-truth evaluation with developer assessments. Our findings suggest that hybrid prompting with architectural context can assist backlog generation from early mockups, though results vary by item type and developer oversight remains necessary.

</details>


### [36] [The Scaffold Effect in Coding Agents: Harness Choice as a Hidden Variable in Coding-Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22585)
*Naman Vats,Oleg Golev*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 7 pages, 2 figures, 6 tables. Preliminary work; under review at the 5th DL4C Workshop @ ICML 2026

Selection reason: 直接研究coding agent harness的影响，对agent评估和设计有重要启发

TL;DR: 编码代理评估中，框架选择对效率影响巨大（令牌差异达40倍），但对通过率影响较小（0-8个百分点）；存在框架级偏差。建议根据令牌/延迟预算选择框架-模型对，并报告完整规格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码代理排行榜常忽略框架差异，导致模型比较不完整，需量化框架对性能和效率的影响。

Method: 在50任务子集上，用三个开源框架评估两个模型，比较通过率和令牌消耗，分析失败模式。

Result: 框架选择导致令牌差异40倍，通过率差异0-8个百分点；错误模式模型无关，框架特有。

Conclusion: 编码代理评估应以框架-模型对为单位，根据成本预算选择，并报告令牌、延迟和框架规格。

中文摘要：针对编码代理的公开排行榜通常按模型名称和通过率对系统进行排名，而周围的框架（即提供工具、管理上下文并决定何时停止的脚手架）往往规定不足。当框架固定时，模型间的比较是有效的；当框架变化时，性能和效率会混淆模型和框架的影响。我们在Terminal-Bench Pro的分层50任务子集上，跨三个开源框架（Goose、OpenCode、OpenHands-SDK）评估了Qwen 3.6 Plus和MiniMax M2.5。框架选择导致每个解决任务的令牌数差异高达40倍，而配对模型内通过率差异保持在0-8个百分点（95%配对任务自举置信区间除最大差距外均包含零）。失败指纹在模型间重复出现（Goose为REASON，OpenHands-SDK为VERIFY/MAX_TURNS，OpenCode为空闲循环/TIME），表明存在很大程度上与模型无关的框架级偏差。对于以人为中心的编码代理评估，仅模型名称是不完整的比较单位：框架-模型对决定了实际成本、延迟和监督负担；无动作回合是每个任务的等待税，而不仅仅是令牌税。因此，我们建议根据令牌/延迟预算下的通过率选择框架-模型对，并在任何模型比较中报告令牌使用量、延迟和完整的框架规格。我们发布了匿名化配置、原始试验日志、聚合快照和分析脚本。

Original abstract: Public leaderboards for coding agents typically rank systems by model name and pass rate, while the surrounding harness (the scaffold that issues tools, manages context, and decides when to stop) is often under-specified. Model-to-model comparison is valid when the harness is fixed; when it varies, performance and efficiency conflate model and scaffold effects. We evaluate Qwen 3.6 Plus and MiniMax M2.5 across three open-source harnesses (Goose, OpenCode, OpenHands-SDK) on a stratified 50-task subset of Terminal-Bench Pro. Harness choice induces up to a 40x difference in tokens per solved task, while paired within-model pass-rate differences remain 0-8 percentage points (95% paired-task bootstrap CIs include zero except for the largest gap). Failure fingerprints replicate across models (REASON for Goose, VERIFY/MAX_TURNS for OpenHands-SDK, idle-loop/TIME for OpenCode), indicating harness-level biases that are largely model-independent. For human-centered coding-agent evaluation, model name alone is an incomplete comparison unit: harness-model pairs determine real-world cost, latency, and oversight burden; no-action turns are a per-task wait tax, not just a token tax. We therefore recommend selecting harness-model pairs by pass rate under token/latency budgets, and reporting token usage, latency, and full harness specifications alongside any model comparison. We release anonymized configs, raw trial logs, aggregated snapshots, and analysis scripts.

</details>


### [37] [ParBench: A Benchmark for Reliable Evaluation of LLM Parallel Code Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22588)
*Samyak Jhaveri,Erel Kaplan,Tom Yotam,Le Chen,Tomer Bitan,Niranjan Hasabnis,Gal Oren*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 57 pages, 22 figures, 11 tables; includes appendices

Selection reason: LLM并行代码翻译基准，直接相关自动代码优化与coding agent

TL;DR: 提出了ParBench基准测试框架，用于评估LLM在跨CUDA、OpenMP、OpenCL、OpenMP target offload等并行API翻译中的行为正确性，发现现有LLM存在翻译方向不对称、多文件协调困难、API适应不完整及对源代码扰动稳健性差等障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在并行代码翻译中缺乏衡量是否保留底层并行语义（如线程索引、同步等）的可靠方法，需要可执行、可重复的基准测试框架。

Method: 设计ParBench框架，通过声明性规范固定构建/运行/验证基础设施，仅翻译计算内核；使用AST驱动、行为保持的源代码增强来测试翻译的稳健性；覆盖CUDA、OpenMP、OpenCL和OpenMP target offload之间的翻译方向。

Result: 对多种LLM的评估显示持续存在的障碍，包括方向不对称、多文件协调、API适应不完整及对扰动的稳健性不均匀。

Conclusion: ParBench揭示了LLM在并行API翻译中的局限性，为未来研究提供了基准和方向。

中文摘要：现代计算密集型软件必须跨越一个由加速器、编程API、编译器栈和可移植性层（包括CUDA、OpenMP、OpenCL和OpenMP target offload）组成的不断变化的生态系统进行迁移。大型语言模型和自主编码智能体日益被提出用于此类迁移，但该领域缺乏可靠的方法来衡量它们是否保留了使翻译在行为上有效的底层并行语义，包括线程索引、同步、内存管理、主机-设备协调以及特定于API的执行结构。我们提出了ParBench，这是一个以内核为中心的基准测试框架，用于在可执行、可重复的条件下评估基于LLM的并行API翻译。ParBench通过声明性基准测试规范固定了周围的构建、运行和验证基础设施，并要求模型仅翻译计算内核。它利用多个开源HPC套件，涵盖了CUDA、OpenMP、OpenCL和OpenMP target offload之间代表性的跨API翻译方向。为了测试成功是否反映了稳健的翻译而非表面形式的记忆，ParBench包括了AST驱动的、预期行为保持的、基线验证的源代码增强。对最先进的开源和专有LLM的评估表明，可靠的并行代码翻译存在持续障碍，包括方向不对称、多文件协调、不完整的API适应以及对源代码级扰动的稳健性不均匀。代码可在https://github.com/Scientific-Computing-Lab/ParBench获取。

Original abstract: Modern compute-intensive software must migrate across a changing ecosystem of accelerators, programming APIs, compiler stacks, and portability layers, including CUDA, OpenMP, OpenCL, and OpenMP target offload. Large language models and autonomous coding agents are increasingly proposed for such migration, but the field lacks reliable ways to measure whether they preserve the low-level parallel semantics that make translations behaviorally valid, including thread indexing, synchronization, memory management, host-device coordination, and API-specific execution structure.
  We present ParBench, a kernel-centric benchmark framework for evaluating LLM-based parallel API translation under executable, reproducible conditions. ParBench fixes the surrounding build, run, and verification infrastructure through declarative benchmark specifications and asks models to translate only the computational kernels. It draws on multiple open-source HPC suites and covers representative cross-API translation directions among CUDA, OpenMP, OpenCL, and OpenMP target offload. To test whether success reflects robust translation rather than surface-form memorization, ParBench includes AST-driven, intended behavior-preserving, baseline-validated source augmentation. Evaluations on state-of-the-art open and proprietary LLMs show persistent barriers to reliable parallel code translation, including direction asymmetry, multi-file coordination, incomplete API adaptation, and uneven robustness to source-level perturbations. Code is available at https://github.com/Scientific-Computing-Lab/ParBench.

</details>


### [38] [Lexical discovery in unknown environments orchestrated by Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22591)
*Rafael Sendra-Arranz,Iñaki Dellibarda Varela,Eduardo Rocon,Álvaro Gutiérrez,Manuel Cebrian*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 32 pages, 7 figures

Selection reason: 多LLM agent自组织词汇发现，通用多agent协作方法可迁移

TL;DR: 提出了神经符号词汇发现框架，让基于LLM的智能体群体通过指称游戏自组织共享的外星词汇，并在模拟中达成共识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在未知环境中自主智能体群体需要共享词汇来指代无名称实体，但现有方法无法自主发现词汇。

Method: 提出NSLD框架，每个智能体使用CLIP视觉编码器、FAISS索引和LLM，通过指称游戏自组织共享的词汇，并将发现的外星词汇锚定到自然语言。

Result: 模拟中多达20个智能体和10个视觉指称物成功达成共识，通过三个分析模型表征收敛动态，R²>0.95。

Conclusion: 这是向自主探索任务中部署前规划的第一步，表明该框架有效。

中文摘要：在未知环境（如行星或深海探测）中部署的自主智能体群体必须发展共享词汇表，以指代任何人类语言中都没有名称的实体。我们提出了神经符号词汇发现（NSLD）框架，其中一群基于LLM的智能体在分布外视觉指称物上玩指称游戏，自主自组织出一个共享的外星词汇表。每个智能体结合了冻结的CLIP视觉编码器、一个私有的FAISS向量索引和一个纯文本LLM。关键的是，发现的外星词汇通过嵌入空间中的语义邻近性锚定到自然语言，从而用新的感知基础词汇扩展人类词汇。在多达二十个智能体和十个视觉指称物的模拟中达成了共识。收敛动态通过三个分析模型进行了表征，R^2 > 0.95，代表着向自主探索任务中部署前规划迈出的第一步。

Original abstract: Populations of autonomous agents deployed in unknown environments (e.g. planetary or deep-sea exploration) must develop shared vocabularies to refer to entities that have no name in any human language. We propose the Neuro-Symbolic Lexical Discovery (NSLD) framework, in which a population of LLM-based agents plays a referential game over out-of-distribution visual referents, autonomously self-organising a shared alien lexicon. Each agent combines a frozen CLIP vision encoder with a private FAISS vector index and a text-only LLM. Crucially, discovered alien words are anchored to natural language via semantic proximity in the embedding space, enlarging the human vocabulary with new perceptually grounded words. Consensus is reached in simulations with populations of up to twenty agents and ten visual referents. Convergence dynamics are characterised through three analytical models achieving R^2 > 0.95, representing a first step towards pre-deployment planning in autonomous exploration missions.

</details>


### [39] [An Agentic Orchestration of Atomistic Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22596)
*Rahul Somasundaram,Adela Habib,Khanh Dang,Sachin Shivakumar,Ryley G. Hill,Golo Wimmer,Avanish Mishra,Aleksandra Pachalieva,Arthur Lui,Hari Viswanathan,Michael Grosskopf,Saryu Fensin,Russell Bent,Nathan DeBardeleben,Earl Lawrence*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 19 pages, 9 figures

Selection reason: 原子模拟的agent编排框架，通用agent架构和工作流自动化可迁移

TL;DR: 提出了一个嵌入URSA框架的基于代理的系统，能够自动化原子模拟的设计、执行和验证，减少人工干预并提高可重复性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原子模拟工作流程复杂且需要大量人工专业知识，现有方法缺乏自动化，导致效率低、可重复性差。

Method: 基于URSA框架的代理系统，自主选择势函数、构建运行模拟，并闭环迭代错误恢复；使用LAVA进行基准测试。

Result: 系统成功自动化LAMMPS模拟，输出与LAVA工具包一致，减少了人工干预和试错。

Conclusion: 该框架提高了原子建模的严谨性、可重复性和可扩展性，有望加速材料设计。

中文摘要：原子模拟对于材料设计至关重要，但其执行涉及复杂、多步骤的工作流程，需要大量人类专业知识。在此，我们提出一个基于代理的系统，嵌入在URSA（通用研究与科学代理）框架中，自动化原子模拟的设计、执行和验证，并使用大规模原子/分子大规模并行模拟器（LAMMPS）工具进行演示。我们的系统自主选择原子间势，构建并运行模拟，并在闭环工作流程中执行迭代错误恢复。我们通过将其输出与LAVA（一种用于LAMMPS和维也纳从头算模拟包（VASP）计算的高通量工具包）进行基准测试，来评估该代理的科学可靠性。我们的框架减少了人工干预和试错，从而提高了原子建模的严谨性、可重复性和可扩展性。

Original abstract: Atomistic simulations are central to materials design, but their execution involves complex, multi-step workflows that require significant human expertise. Here, we present an agent-based system embedded within the URSA (Universal Research and Scientific Agent) framework that automates the design, execution, and validation of atomistic simulations, demonstrated using the Large-scale Atomic/Molecular Massively Parallel Simulator (LAMMPS) tool. Our system autonomously selects interatomic potentials, constructs and runs simulations, and performs iterative error recovery within a closed-loop workflow. We evaluate the scientific reliability of the agent by benchmarking its outputs against LAVA, a high-throughput toolkit for LAMMPS and the Vienna Ab initio Simulation Package (VASP) calculations. Our framework reduces manual intervention and trial-and-error, thereby improving the rigor, reproducibility, and scalability of atomistic modeling.

</details>


### [40] [Chart Deception in Vision-Language Models: From Vulnerability to Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22600)
*Ridwan Mahbub,Mohammed Saidul Islam,Md Tahmid Rahman Laskar,Mizanur Rahman,Mir Tafseer Nayeem,Enamul Hoque*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 多agent缓解框架用于VLM图表欺骗，多agent协作方法有一般性启发

TL;DR: 提出VisDeception基准和Deception Score来评估VLM对欺骗性图表的鲁棒性，并设计多智能体框架提高鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM用于图表理解，但对欺骗性可视化设计缺乏鲁棒性评估，需要可靠的评估方法和缓解策略。

Method: 构建包含1,600对忠实/欺骗性图表的基准，覆盖8类欺骗策略；提出配对评估指标Deception Score；设计多智能体推理框架，利用图表元数据减少欺骗影响。

Result: 在10个先进VLM上的32,000个响应表明，即使是先进模型也易受欺骗；多智能体框架可提升鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 揭示可靠性差距，建立基准、度量、结构化推理方向。

中文摘要：信息可视化被广泛用于沟通模式、趋势和异常值，然而欺骗性设计选择——例如截断或反转坐标轴、扭曲的长宽比、不恰当的编码和误导性的颜色映射——可以在保持底层数据不变的情况下系统性地改变解释。随着视觉语言模型(VLMs)越来越多地被用于图表理解和分析推理，评估它们对此类欺骗性可视化的鲁棒性对于可信的数据分析变得至关重要。我们引入了VisDeception，这是第一个用于评估VLMs对误导性图表设计鲁棒性的受控配对基准。该基准包含1,600对忠实和误导性图表，涵盖八大类欺骗性可视化策略，其中每个误导性图表都与基于相同底层数据生成的忠实图表配对。为了隔离由欺骗引起的推理错误与基线图表理解错误，我们引入了欺骗分数(Deception Score)，这是一种配对评估指标，用于量化误导性可视化如何将模型响应从对数据的忠实解释中偏移。在来自10个最先进VLMs的32,000个响应中，我们发现即使是高级模型仍然高度容易受到欺骗性视觉操纵的影响。为了提高鲁棒性，我们进一步提出了一种推理时的多智能体缓解框架，该框架在答案生成之前将推理基于从可视化中提取的结构化图表元数据，从而使模型能够减少欺骗性视觉线索的影响，而无需明确的用户指令。总之，我们的发现揭示了当前图表理解系统中重要的可靠性差距，并建立了基准驱动的评估、欺骗感知度量和结构化推理作为开发更可信的用于视觉分析的VLMs的有前途的方向。

Original abstract: Information visualizations are widely used to communicate patterns, trends, and outliers, yet deceptive design choices-such as truncated or inverted axes, distorted aspect ratios, inappropriate encodings, and misleading color mappings-can systematically alter interpretation while preserving the underlying data. As Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly used for chart understanding and analytical reasoning, assessing their robustness to such deceptive visualizations has become critical for trustworthy data analysis. We introduce VisDeception, the first controlled paired benchmark for evaluating the robustness of VLMs to misleading chart designs. The benchmark contains 1,600 paired faithful and misleading charts spanning eight major categories of deceptive visualization tactics, where each misleading chart is paired with a faithful counterpart generated from the same underlying data. To isolate deception-induced reasoning errors from baseline chart-understanding errors, we introduce the Deception Score, a paired evaluation metric that quantifies how misleading visualizations shift model responses away from the faithful interpretation of the data. Across 32,000 responses from 10 state-of-the-art VLMs, we find that even advanced models remain highly vulnerable to deceptive visual manipulations. To improve robustness, we further propose an inference-time multi-agent mitigation framework that grounds reasoning in structured chart metadata extracted from the visualization before answer generation, enabling models to reduce the influence of deceptive visual cues without requiring explicit user instructions. Together, our findings reveal important reliability gaps in current chart-understanding systems and establish benchmark-driven evaluation, deception-aware metrics, and structured reasoning as promising directions for developing more trustworthy VLMs for visual analytics.

</details>


### [41] [DeepLook: Deeper Thinking with Lookahead](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22602)
*Tingxin Yang,Zefeng Wang,Mengyue Wang,Xingcheng Zhou,Yunpu Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 推理时监控与干预策略，对agent推理改进有参考价值

TL;DR: 提出DeepLook框架，通过监控-干预在不确定性瓶颈处集中前瞻计算，显著改善推理准确率-成本权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理时扩展方法计算分配低效，且推理失败往往早期出现不确定性。

Method: 训练自由的监控-干预解码框架：聚合词元级置信度为段落级信号，置信度下降时触发固定前瞻探索，用平均前瞻置信度（ALC）排名分支并投票选择。

Result: 在四个数学基准上，16个设置中11个提升准确率，平均减少87.3%词元生成；如AIME25上+3.1，BRUMO25上+8.8。

Conclusion: 选择性未来感知干预比统一扩展整个推理轨迹产生更好的准确率-成本权衡。

中文摘要：推理时扩展已成为改进大型语言模型推理的强大范式，通常在困难推理任务上比仅靠参数扩展带来更大的收益。然而，现有方法在推理轨迹内分配计算资源方面仍然低效。受推理失败往往在错误答案变得明确之前就早期出现不确定性这一观察的启发，我们引入了DeepLook，一种无需训练的监控-干预解码框架，将前瞻计算集中在不确定性瓶颈处。DeepLook将词元级置信度聚合为段落级信号，当置信度相对于近期历史下降时触发，并用固定水平的前瞻探索候选延续。分支通过平均前瞻置信度（ALC）进行排名，ALC是滚动延续上的平均段落级置信度，然后通过投票进行剪枝和聚合。在四个竞赛级数学基准上（DeepSeek-R1-8B、Qwen3-32B、GPT-OSS-20B和GPT-OSS-120B），DeepLook改变了准确率-词元成本帕累托前沿：在16个设置中的11个中，它比DeepConf-low提高了准确率，同时平均将数据集级词元生成减少了87.3%，包括Qwen3-32B在AIME25上+3.1的提升和GPT-OSS-20B在BRUMO25上+8.8的提升。这些结果表明，选择性的、面向未来的干预比统一扩展完整推理轨迹产生了更强大的准确率-成本权衡。代码可在此处获取。

Original abstract: Inference-time scaling has emerged as a powerful paradigm for improving large language model reasoning, often delivering larger gains on difficult reasoning tasks than parameter scaling alone. However, existing approaches remain inefficient in how compute is allocated within a reasoning trace. Motivated by the observation that reasoning failures often exhibit an early onset of uncertainty before a wrong answer become explicit, we introduce DeepLook, a training-free monitor-and-intervene decoding framework that concentrates lookahead compute at uncertainty bottlenecks. DeepLook aggregates token-level confidence into segment-level signals, triggers when confidence drops relative to recent history, and explores candidate continuations with fixed-horizon lookahead. Branches are ranked by Average Lookahead Confidence (ALC), the average segment-level confidence over rollout continuations, then pruned and aggregated through voting. On four competition-style mathematics benchmarks across DeepSeek-R1-8B, Qwen3-32B, GPT-OSS-20B, and GPT-OSS-120B, DeepLook shifts the accuracy--token-cost Pareto frontier: it improves accuracy over DeepConf-low in 11 of 16 settings while reducing dataset-level token generation by 87.3% on average, including gains of +3.1 on AIME25 with Qwen3-32B and +8.8 on BRUMO25 with GPT-OSS-20B. These results show that selective, future-aware intervention yields substantially stronger accuracy--cost trade-offs than uniformly scaling complete reasoning trajectories. Code is available here.

</details>


### [42] [Evaluating LLMs as Interpretable Controllers for Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22609)
*Aleksander Østensen,Alberto Mino Calero,Anastasios M. Lekkas,Adil Rasheed*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: LLM作为可解释控制器，对agent决策评估有一般意义

TL;DR: 本文探索了LLMs作为动态热环境可解释控制器的能力，发现高复杂度模型能准确跟踪温度并提供物理一致的解释，结合物理模型可提升能效和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注LLMs在决策和推理任务上的应用，但对其作为物理系统控制器的潜力探索不足，因此本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在动态热环境中测试五种不同规模的LLM，评估其遵循设定点、解释自然语言命令、推理执行器效应及整合物理模型的能力。

Result: 低/中规模模型常误解执行器动态；高复杂度模型（如Qwen-3~14B和GPT-4o）实现精确温度控制与物理一致解释；结合物理模型显著提升控制平稳性和能效。

Conclusion: 足够强大且基于领域知识的LLMs可作为可解释控制器，为混合模型与语言驱动控制策略提供新思路。

中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLMs）越来越多地被用于决策和推理任务，但它们作为物理系统控制器的潜力在很大程度上尚未被探索。这项工作研究了LLMs是否可以作为动态热环境的可解释控制器，考察它们遵循设定点、解释自然语言命令、推理执行器效应以及整合基于模型的先验知识的能力。在多种场景下评估了五种不同规模的LLM，包括对加热器或风扇使用施加惩罚的设置，以及模型可以访问基于物理的预测工具的情况。结果表明，控制性能取决于模型复杂度：低规模和中等规模的模型经常误解执行器动态或产生不一致的推理，而高复杂度的模型（如Qwen-3~14B和GPT-4o）实现了准确的温度跟踪、稳定的执行器使用以及与物理原理一致的连贯解释。结合基于物理的模型通过实现预期决策显著提高了控制的平稳性和能效。详细的推理分类进一步揭示了从较小模型中的因果误解到较大模型中连贯且具有时间意识的推理的清晰进展。这些发现表明，当LLMs足够强大并适当地基于领域知识时，它们可以作为可解释的控制器，突显了混合基于模型和语言驱动控制策略的前景，这些策略可以提供合理的解释。

Original abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used for decision-making and reasoning tasks, yet their potential as controllers for physical systems remains largely unexplored. This work investigates whether LLMs can function as interpretable controllers for a dynamic thermal environment, examining their ability to follow setpoints, interpret natural-language commands, reason about actuator effects, and incorporate prior model-based knowledge. Five LLMs of varying scales are evaluated under multiple scenarios, including settings with penalties on heater or fan usage and cases where the models have access to a physics-based prediction tool. The results show that control performance depends on model complexity: while low- and mid-scale models frequently misinterpret actuator dynamics or generate inconsistent reasoning, high-complexity models such as Qwen-3~14B and GPT-4o achieve accurate temperature tracking, stable actuator usage, and coherent explanations aligned with physical principles. Incorporating a physics-based model significantly improves control smoothness and energy efficiency by enabling anticipatory decision-making. A detailed reasoning taxonomy further reveals a clear progression from causal misinterpretation in smaller models to cohesive and temporally aware reasoning in larger ones. The findings demonstrate that LLMs can act as interpretable controllers when sufficiently capable and appropriately grounded in domain knowledge, highlighting promising opportunities for hybrid model-based and language-driven control strategies that can provide plausible explanations.

</details>


### [43] [Tokengeist: Multi-Turn Attribution Tracing in Agentic Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22610)
*Jessica Tang,Shraddha Barke,Sharad Agarwal*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 多轮对话归因追踪，对agent记忆与推理的上下文利用有启发

TL;DR: 提出多轮上下文归因任务及Tokengeist框架，通过递归遍历DAG恢复多跳依赖，构建MTCABench基准，在四个模型上达到90%源召回率，揭示了单次归因的失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文归因方法只捕捉表面级别依赖，无法处理多轮对话的分层、非线性结构。

Method: 定义多轮上下文归因（MTCA）任务，提出Tokengeist框架，将归因视为对话轮次上有向无环图的递归遍历，并构建MTCABench基准。

Result: 平面归因方法源召回率低于20%，Tokengeist达到90%，揭示了单次归因的系统性失败模式“来源坍缩”。

Conclusion: 多轮对话归因需要跨轮次递归推理的方法，Tokengeist有效恢复多跳依赖。

中文摘要：当语言模型在多轮对话中生成回复时，哪些来自先前轮次的标记塑造了该答案，以及这些依赖关系如何跨先前轮次传播？现有的上下文归因方法在一次处理中处理整个上下文，恢复了表面级别的依赖关系，但错过了现实世界对话和多步推理任务的分层、非线性结构。我们引入了多轮上下文归因（MTCA）：给定模型回复中的一个目标片段，任务是向后跨轮次追踪归因，不仅识别哪些先前轮次直接相关，而且识别这些轮次本身如何依赖于更早的上下文。我们提出了Tokengeist，一个与归因方法无关且可扩展的框架，通过将归因视为对话轮次上有向无环图（DAG）的递归遍历来恢复完整的依赖路径。我们将发布MTCABench，一个包含665个多轮对话中的3,845个目标片段的基准测试，注释了深度达14的黄金来源图，跨越四种依赖类型。在四个开源模型上，平面归因方法未能恢复多跳依赖，源召回率低于20%，而Tokengeist达到90%。我们的结果揭示了单次归因的系统性失败模式——我们称之为来源坍缩——并激励了跨轮次递归推理的归因方法。

Original abstract: When a language model produces a response in a multi-turn conversation, which tokens from prior turns shaped that answer, and how did those dependencies propagate across prior turns? Existing context attribution methods process the full context in a single pass, recovering surface-level dependencies but missing the layered, non-linear structure of real-world dialogues and multi-step reasoning tasks. We introduce multi-turn context attribution (MTCA): given a target span in a model response, the task of tracing attribution backward across turns to identify not only which prior turns were directly relevant, but also how those turns themselves depended on earlier context. We propose Tokengeist, an attribution-method-agnostic and scalable framework that recovers full dependency paths by casting attribution as a recursive traversal of a directed acyclic graph (DAG) over conversation turns. We will release MTCABench, a benchmark of 3,845 target spans across 665 multi-turn conversations, annotated with gold provenance graphs reaching depths of up to 14, across four dependency types. Across four open-weight models, flat attribution methods fail to recover multi-hop dependencies, achieving under 20% source recall, while Tokengeist reaches 90%. Our results reveal systematic failure modes of single-pass attribution -- which we term provenance collapse -- and motivate attribution methods that reason recursively across turns.

</details>


### [44] [Decentralized Granular Access Control for Agentic AI Systems in Critical Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22611)
*Arun Malik,Deepal Jayasinghe,Bradley Klemick,Prachi Shah,Nitish Talasu,Vineet Tushar Trivedi*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 7 pages, 9 figures, 7 tables

Selection reason: agentic AI的访问控制架构，对agent安全部署有参考价值

TL;DR: 提出一种针对云基础设施中AI代理的去中心化多层访问控制架构，通过复合身份、分层权限、去中心化策略和渐进信任升级，在八个月生产中实现零未授权写操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RBAC模型无法应对AI代理的随机行为，导致其访问关键系统时存在安全风险。

Method: 框架包含四项创新：复合身份模型、五级层次权限系统、去中心化策略所有权模型、渐进信任升级与安全联锁，并基于OWASP威胁分类设计。

Result: 在主要云提供商的数百个数据中心生产环境中部署，管理20+ AI代理和60+确定性剧本，每天处理数千操作，八个月零未授权写操作。

Conclusion: 实验数据表明，该架构通过分层授权有效防止了非确定性行为者的权限提升，保障了基础设施安全。

中文摘要：在自主AI代理部署于生产基础设施时，引入了传统基于角色的访问控制（RBAC）模型无法解决的根本性安全挑战。与确定性自动化不同，AI代理表现出随机行为，使得传统信任模型不足以管理其对关键系统的访问。本文提出了一种专为在关键云基础设施中运行的代理AI系统设计的去中心化多层访问控制架构。我们的框架引入了四项关键创新：(1)一种复合身份模型，将代理动作绑定到委托的人类权威，(2)一个五级粒度层级权限系统，从全局平台访问到每个参数约束，(3)一个去中心化策略所有权模型，工具团队独立管理其授权边界，以及(4)渐进信任升级与安全联锁，防止自主代理执行高风险操作。我们将设计基于OWASP十大LLM应用威胁分类（2025），并展示每个架构决策如何缓解特定攻击向量。该系统已在管理数百个数据中心网络基础设施的主要云提供商的生产环境中部署，为20多个专业AI代理和60多个确定性剧本强制执行细粒度访问控制，每天处理数千次操作，同时在八个月的生产部署中保持零未授权写操作。我们提供了访问模式分布、拒绝率以及分层授权在防止非确定性行为者权限提升方面的有效性的经验数据。

Original abstract: The deployment of autonomous AI agents in production infrastructure introduces fundamental security challenges that traditional role-based access control (RBAC) models cannot address. Unlike deterministic automation, AI agents exhibit stochastic behavior, making conventional trust models insufficient for governing their access to critical systems. This paper presents a decentralized, multi-layered access control architecture designed specifically for agentic AI systems operating in critical cloud infrastructure. Our framework introduces four key innovations: (1) a compound identity model that binds agent actions to delegated human authority, (2) a hierarchical permission system spanning five granularity levels from global platform access to per-parameter constraints, (3) a decentralized policy ownership model where tool teams independently govern their authorization boundaries, and (4) progressive trust escalation with safety interlocks that prevent autonomous agents from executing high-risk operations. We ground our design in the OWASP Top 10 for LLM Applications (2025) threat taxonomy and demonstrate how each architectural decision mitigates specific attack vectors. Deployed in production at a major cloud provider managing network infrastructure across hundreds of datacenters, the system enforces granular access control for 20+ specialized AI agents and 60+ deterministic playbooks processing thousands of operations daily while maintaining zero unauthorized write operations over eight months of production deployment. We present empirical data on access pattern distributions, denial rates, and the effectiveness of layered authorization in preventing privilege escalation by non-deterministic actors.

</details>


### [45] [Opti-Q: A Constraint-Based Optimization Framework for Multi-LLM Question Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22621)
*Aamir Hamid,Bharg Barot,Satvik Racharla,Tim Finin,Primal Pappachan,Roberto Yus*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 多LLM编排的规划框架，可迁移至agent规划方法。

TL;DR: OPTI-Q是一个数据库启发的成本优化器，通过将LLM调用建模为DAG算子并使用统计目录估计成本与质量，在预算约束下搜索帕累托最优计划，显著提升了多LLM问答的质量-资源权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型部署时面临非确定性和异构资源（成本、延迟、能耗）的挑战，需要一种能够在预算约束下优化回答质量的规划方法。

Method: 将LLM调用建模为执行DAG中的物理算子，使用PERFDB统计目录估计算子及子计划的质量与资源成本，通过帕累托前沿搜索根据用户偏好选择最优计划。

Result: 在MMLU-Pro和SimpleQA数据集上，在用户指定预算下，OPTI-Q将平均回答质量分别提高了约58%和约41%，同时成本与基线相当。

Conclusion: 数据库风格的规划能够为多LLM问答系统实现更好的质量-资源权衡，验证了其有效性。

中文摘要：尽管大型语言模型（LLM）能够支持强大的问答（QA）能力，但预算受限的部署因非确定性和异构资源概况（成本、延迟和能耗）而变得复杂。我们提出OPTI-Q，一种受数据库启发的基于成本的优化器，实现了多LLM编排的“先计划后执行”范式。OPTI-Q将LLM调用建模为执行DAG中的物理算子，并为每个问题搜索能够在用户指定的资源约束下优化回答质量（QoA）同时权衡财务成本、延迟和能耗的计划。计划可以包括顺序算子（将中间答案作为上下文传递）以及并行/混合算子（并发运行模型并合并输出）。为了在不执行每个候选计划的情况下搜索这一空间，OPTI-Q使用PERFDB（一个从基准测试和执行轨迹中填充并刷新的统计目录）来估计单个算子和组合子计划的QoA与资源成本。利用这些估计，OPTI-Q执行帕累托前沿搜索，并根据用户偏好选择最终计划。在MMLU-Pro和SimpleQA上，在用户指定预算下，OPTI-Q在可比成本下将平均QoA相比于基线提高了约58%和约41%，证明了数据库风格的规划为多LLM问答带来了更好的质量-资源权衡。

Original abstract: While large language models (LLMs) enable strong question answering (QA), budgeted deployment is complicated by nondeterminism and heterogeneous resource profiles (cost, latency, and energy). We present OPTI-Q, a database-inspired, cost-based optimizer that implements a plan-before-execute paradigm for multi-LLM orchestration. OPTI-Q models LLM invocations as physical operators in an execution DAG and, for each question, searches for plans that optimize answer quality (QoA) while trading off financial cost, latency, and energy under user-specified resource constraints. Plans can include sequential operators that pass intermediate answers as context and parallel/blend operators that run models concurrently and merge their outputs. To search this space without executing each candidate plan, OPTI-Q uses PERFDB, a statistics catalog populated and refreshed from benchmarks and execution traces, to estimate the QoA and resource costs of both individual operators and composed subplans. Using these estimates, OPTI-Q performs Pareto-frontier search and selects a final plan based on user preferences. On MMLU-Pro and SimpleQA under user-specified budgets, OPTI-Q improves average QoA by ~58% and ~41% over baselines at comparable cost, demonstrating that database-style planning yields better quality-resource trade-offs for multi-LLM QA.

</details>


### [46] [Evolving from Lessons: Skill-Augmented Table Graph Reasoning for Operation-wise Table Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22633)
*Guixin Su,Qiankun Pi,Mayi Xu,Wenli Li,Ming Zhong,Yuanyuan Zhu,Jiawei Jiang,Tieyun Qian*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 技能增强表格推理，含技能库和轨迹复用，可迁移至agent技能学习。

TL;DR: 提出SkillTGR框架，通过将表格表示为属性图并利用技能库进行对比增强推理，显著提升了LLM在复杂表格问答上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法忽视不同操作难度差异，且线性化表格破坏结构导致复杂推理困难，同时缺乏对可重用推理模式的利用。

Method: 将表格建模为属性图，由LLM动态规划证据子图检索路径；构建分层SkillBank存储和检索抽象技能，并通过对比增强结合成功与失败案例进行推理。

Result: 在WikiTQ-ow和TabFact-ow上整体准确率提升5.91%，操作级提升6.03%，同时token消耗减少19.76%，推理延迟降低27.64%。

Conclusion: SkillTGR通过结构化图推理和技能蒸馏实现了高效的表格问答，验证了操作级评估和技能增强方法的有效性。

中文摘要：表格问答旨在对表格进行推理以回答用户查询。现有研究对所有问题一视同仁，仅通过整体准确率进行评估，掩盖了一个关键现实：大型语言模型擅长简单查询，但在聚合和算术等复杂操作上表现不佳。为了揭示这种差异，我们引入了一项新的操作级表格问答任务，并提供了细粒度的问题分类法，同时发布了两个数据集 WikiTQ-ow 和 TabFact-ow 用于评估。关于建模瓶颈，现有方法将表格展平为线性化文本，破坏了固有结构并引发了“中间丢失”问题，这成为复杂跨行推理的主要障碍。此外，它们通常从零开始推理，忽略了跨相似操作共享的可重用模式。为了解决这些局限性，我们提出了一种技能增强的表格图推理框架，用于自我进化的结构化推理。具体而言，SkillTGR 将表格表示为具有显式行-列-单元格结构的属性图，LLM 在其中规划和执行动态链以检索证据子图进行图遍历推理。基于此，SkillTGR 构建了一个分层技能库，在认知启发下将推理轨迹蒸馏为抽象技能，然后混合检索成功和失败的技能进行对比增强的表格图推理，从而实现持续自我进化。大量实验表明，SkillTGR 取得了优越的性能，平均整体提升 5.91%，操作级提升 6.03%，同时减少了 19.76% 的 token 消耗和 27.64% 的推理延迟。我们的代码和数据将在发表后发布。

Original abstract: Table Question Answering (TableQA) aims to reason over tables to answer user queries. Existing research treats all questions uniformly and evaluates solely through overall accuracy, obscuring a critical reality that LLMs excel at simple lookups yet struggle with complex operations like aggregation and arithmetic. To reveal this disparity, we introduce a novel \emph{Operation-wise TableQA} task with a fine-grained question taxonomy and release two datasets named WikiTQ-ow and TabFact-ow for evaluation. As for modeling bottlenecks, existing methods flatten tables into linearized texts, disrupting inherent structures and inducing the ``lost-in-the-middle'' issue, which poses a primary barrier to complex cross-row reasoning. Moreover, they typically reason from scratch, neglecting reusable patterns shared across similar operations. To address these limitations, we propose a Skill-augmented Table Graph Reasoning (SkillTGR) framework for self-evolving structured reasoning. Specifically, SkillTGR represents tables as attributed graphs with explicit row-column-cell structures, where LLMs plan and execute dynamic chains to retrieve evidence subgraphs for graph traversal reasoning. Based on this, SkillTGR builds a hierarchical SkillBank to distill reason trajectories into abstract skills under cognitive heuristics, then hybrid retrieves both successful and failed skills for contrastive augmented table graph reasoning, thereby enabling the continual self-evolution. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SkillTGR achieves superior performance with an average of 5.91\% overall and 6.03\% operation-wise improvement, also reducing 19.76\% token consumption and 27.64\% inference latency. Our codes and data will be released upon publication.

</details>


### [47] [CallBench: A Benchmark for Dual-Goal Coordination in Phone Call Assistants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22635)
*Xuzhao Geng,Haozhao Wang,Xuelian Li,Zhenyu Yang,Haonan Lu,Rui Zhang,Ruixuan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 电话助手双目标协调基准，与agent评测相关。

TL;DR: 提出了CallBench，一个中文基准，用于评估电话助手中协调设备所有者预设目标与呼叫者动态目标的双目标对话能力，实验显示现有方法仍存在挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面向目标的对话系统仅针对单一显式目标，而电话助手需要协调设备所有者的预设目标与呼叫者的隐式动态目标，缺乏相关基准和方法。

Method: 构建了包含50,000个多轮电话对话的CallBench基准，覆盖六个场景、三种预设类型和四种目标关系，并设计了一个预设感知的轮次级评估协议。

Result: 在代表性对话方法上的实验表明，现有方法在双目标协调任务上表现不佳，难以做出可靠的轮次级决策。

Conclusion: 需要开发能够可靠协调两个独立目标并满足代理约束的电话助手。

中文摘要：面向目标的对话系统已展示出通过交互式对话完成用户目标的强大能力。然而，现有研究主要针对单一显式目标完成，而电话助手面临一种代理场景，需要协调设备所有者的显式预设目标与呼叫者的隐式动态目标。我们引入了CallBench，一个用于评估电话助手中双目标协调的中文基准。CallBench包含50,000个完整的多轮电话对话，涵盖六个场景：外卖、快递、出租车、工作、生活和骚扰。它覆盖了常规预设、突发预设和无预设情况，并包含所有者和呼叫者目标之间的多样关系，如一致、互补、无关和冲突。我们进一步设计了一个预设感知的轮次级评估协议，涵盖语义理解、上下文使用、主动引导、响应质量、预设合规、对话节奏和安全性。在代表性对话方法上的实验表明，现有方法仍然难以完成此任务，这突显了对能够在代理约束下在两个独立目标之间做出可靠轮次级决策的电话助手的需求。

Original abstract: Target-oriented dialogue systems have demonstrated strong capabilities in completing user goals through interactive conversations. However, existing studies are primarily designed for single, explicit goal completion, while phone call assistants face a proxy setting that requires coordinating the device owner's explicit preset goal with the caller's implicit and dynamic goal. We introduce \textsc{CallBench}, a Chinese benchmark for evaluating dual-goal coordination in phone call assistants. \textsc{CallBench} contains 50,000 complete multi-turn phone call dialogues across six scenarios: takeout, delivery, taxi, work, life, and harassment. It covers regular presets, emergent presets, and no-preset cases, and includes diverse relations between owner-side and caller-side goals, such as alignment, complementarity, irrelevance, and conflict. We further design a preset-aware turn-level evaluation protocol covering semantic understanding, context use, active guidance, response quality, preset compliance, dialogue rhythm, and safety. Experiments on representative dialogue methods show that existing approaches still struggle with this task, highlighting the need for phone call assistants that can make reliable turn-level decisions between two independent goals under proxy constraints.

</details>


### [48] [TRACE: Business Rule-Grounded Reasoning Curriculum for Knowledge-Preserving Parametric Tool Retrieval in Enterprise LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22639)
*Sai Shruthi Sistla,Ashutosh Hathidara,Christopher Toukmaji,Mayank Shrivastava,Karthikeyan Asokkumar*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 工具检索含思考链，直接涉及agent工具使用和推理。

TL;DR: TRACE通过两阶段课程（记忆SFT+推理痕迹训练）解决了参数化检索中的工具知识破坏和推理速度慢问题，在保持或提升工具理解的同时实现了单束贪心解码的高效检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数化检索的两个关键缺陷：训练破坏工具知识，且束搜索解码太慢不适合实时部署。

Method: 两阶段课程：阶段1用LoRA注入工具知识，阶段2训练模型在生成工具JSON前输出思考痕迹，使用RRB数据和合成业务查询并附推理痕迹，训练目标保持阶段1准确率并允许单束贪心解码。

Result: 在8300+工具的企业目录上，TRACE阶段2相比阶段1：MCQ准确率+3.2pp，QA探测+9pp；检索召回率：领域A约86%，领域B约60%，远超嵌入基线（27%和52%），且使用单束贪心解码。

Conclusion: TRACE有效解决参数化检索的缺陷，实现高效、准确的工具检索，可直接用于生产环境。

中文摘要：参数化检索使得大型语言模型能够通过为每个API分配一个唯一的虚拟令牌，并训练模型通过约束束搜索生成它，从而隐式地检索工具。Toolsense表明，这种机制有两个关键缺陷：它在训练过程中破坏了参数化工具知识，并且其束搜索解码对于实时部署来说太慢。我们提出TRACE（通过增强链式思维和企业规则进行工具检索），这是一个两阶段课程，解决了这种分离问题。阶段1重用来自ToolSense的多格式记忆SFT，通过LoRA注入工具知识。阶段2是我们的核心贡献：模型被训练在生成工具令牌的JSON列表之前发出思考痕迹，使用两个数据源——来自ToolSense的RRB对和针对领域专家策划的业务规则合成的查询——两者都附有推理痕迹。这个训练目标保持了阶段1的MCQ和QA探测准确性，同时实现了使用单束贪心解码的生产延迟。在包含两个企业产品线的8300多个工具的组合企业目录上评估，TRACE的阶段2训练不仅保持而且提高了工具理解：MCQ准确率比阶段1提高了+3.2个百分点，QA探测提高了+9个百分点。在检索方面，TRACE在领域A上实现了约86%的召回率，在领域B上实现了约60%——而嵌入基线性能约为27%和52%——两者都使用单束贪心解码，使其直接可以在生产延迟下部署。

Original abstract: Parametric retrieval enables LLMs to retrieve tools implicitly by assigning each API a unique virtual token and training the model to generate it via constrained beam search. Toolsense shows that this regime has two critical drawbacks: it destroys parametric tool knowledge during training, and its beam-search decoding is too slow for real-time deployment. We introduce TRACE (Tool Retrieval via Augmented Chain-of-thought and Enterprise rules), a two-stage curriculum that resolves this dissociation. Stage 1 reuses the multi-format memorization SFT from ToolSense to seed tool knowledge with LoRA. Stage 2 is our core contribution: the model is trained to emit a thinking trace before producing a JSON list of tool tokens, using two data sources -- RRB pairs from ToolSense and queries synthesized to target business rules curated by domain experts -- both augmented with reasoning traces. This training objective preserves Stage 1 MCQ and QA probing accuracy while enabling single-beam greedy decoding at production latency. Evaluated on a combined enterprise catalog of 8,300+ tools across two enterprise product lines, TRACE training for Stage 2 not only preserves but improves tool understanding: MCQ accuracy gains +3.2 pp and QA probing gains +9 pp over Stage 1. On retrieval, TRACE achieves ~86% recall on Domain A and ~60% on Domain B -- compared to embedding baseline performance of ~27% & ~52% -- both with single-beam greedy decoding, making it directly deployable at production latency.

</details>


### [49] [Reason Before You Retrieve: Agentic Planning for Multi-modal RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22643)
*Tianyu Yang,Shir Simon,Zhenzhen Li,Minhao Cheng,Xiangliang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 多模态检索agent，先推理后检索，可迁移规划方法。

TL;DR: 提出了MM-R2，一种在检索前进行推理的多模态智能体检索框架，通过构建意图接地检索状态和结构化知识地图大幅提升检索增强生成的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有mRAG系统在扁平空间检索，面临检索目标不明确和搜索空间弱结构化的挑战，导致检索效果不佳。

Method: 提出MM-R2框架，先构建意图接地检索状态，再在结构化知识地图上由智能体选择检索单元并发出接地查询；并构建MM-R2-Traj轨迹数据集，结合监督微调和GRPO进行后训练。

Result: 在Infoseek和Encyclopedic VQA数据集上，MM-R2在答案准确性上显著优于强基线，且检索轨迹更可解释和可验证。

Conclusion: MM-R2通过推理驱动检索和结构化知识地图，有效解决了mRAG中的检索难题，提升了性能与可解释性。

中文摘要：多模态检索增强生成（mRAG）旨在通过外部知识回答图像-文本查询，但现有系统大多仍然直接从原始多模态输入在扁平证据空间上进行检索。这种设计通常面临两个关键挑战：检索目标不明确，因为问题意图必须正确接地到视觉指代物；搜索空间结构弱，迫使语义不同的证据在单一的全局排序步骤中竞争。我们提出MM-R2，一种多模态智能体检索框架，在检索之前通过显式建模检索内容和搜索位置来进行推理。MM-R2首先从图像-问题对构建意图接地检索状态，捕获信息需求、接地指代物和检索约束。然后，它在结构化的知识地图（KnowledgeMap）上进行检索，智能体在选择相关检索单元后在其中发出接地查询。为了实现这一能力，我们构建了MM-R2-Traj，一个大规模的多步检索过程轨迹数据集，并采用了两阶段后训练策略，包括监督微调和GRPO。在Infoseek和Encyclopedic VQA数据集上的实验表明，MM-R2在答案准确性上大大优于强基线，同时产生了更可解释和可验证的检索轨迹。

Original abstract: Multimodal retrieval-augmented generation (mRAG) aims to answer image-text queries with external knowledge, but most existing systems still retrieve directly from raw multimodal input over a flat evidence space. This design often struggles with two key challenges: the retrieval target is under-specified because the question intent must be grounded to the correct visual referent, and the search space is weakly structured, forcing semantically distinct evidence to compete in a single global ranking step. We propose MM-R2, a multimodal agentic retrieval framework that reasons before retrieval by explicitly modeling both what to retrieve and where to search. MM-R2 first constructs an intent-grounded retrieval state from the image-question pair, capturing the information need, grounded referent, and retrieval constraints. It then performs retrieval over a structured KnowledgeMap, where the agent selects relevant retrieval units before issuing grounded queries within them. To enable this capability, we build MM-R2-Traj, a large-scale trajectory dataset of multi-step retrieval processes, and adopt a two-stage post-training strategy with supervised fine-tuning and GRPO. Experiments on Infoseek and Encyclopedic VQA datasets show that MM-R2 substantially outperforms strong baselines on answer accuracy while also yielding more interpretable and verifiable retrieval trajectories.

</details>


### [50] [DocHRL: A Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning Framework for Cost-Optimised Document Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22644)
*Mohammed Yousif,Prabhjot Singh,Arjun Pankajakshan,Madhu Reddiboina*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 9 pages, 4 tables, 2 figures

Selection reason: 分层RL用于文档分类，agent决策与成本优化，可迁移。

TL;DR: DocHRL是一个分层强化学习框架，通过自适应选择模型序列，在RVL-CDIP上以低成本达到高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统固定模型序列对所有文档一视同仁，导致简单文档过度计算、复杂文档处理不足，资源利用低效。

Method: 采用两层策略结构的强化学习：顶层策略选择模型类别（视觉分类器、LLM、OCR、人工审核），子策略选择具体模型；奖励为负总成本（推理+误分类+人工标注），使用PPO训练。

Result: 在RVL-CDIP上，宏F1=0.973，平均每文档成本2.74标准化单位，远低于固定分类器。

Conclusion: 成本感知的强化学习能同时提升分类性能和操作效率。

中文摘要：实际的文档分类流水线通常对每个传入的文档应用相同的模型序列，无论其复杂性或类型如何。这导致计算和人力资源的低效使用：简单文档被过度处理，而困难文档可能得不到足够的审查。我们提出了DocHRL，一个分层强化学习框架，它学习在每个文档的基础上自适应地、动态地选择最具成本效益的分类策略。DocHRL将文档分类表述为一个具有两级策略层次的序列决策问题：顶层策略选择广泛的选项（视觉分类器、大语言模型、OCR和人机交互审查），而特定于选项的子策略则选择要调用的具体模型或工具。奖励信号是负的总预期成本，其中包含推理成本、错误分类成本和人工标注成本。在RVL-CDIP基准上使用近端策略优化进行训练，DocHRL在16个文档类别上实现了0.973的宏F1分数，同时将平均每文档成本降低到2.74标准化单位，而固定的独立分类器则产生高得多的成本。我们的结果表明，成本感知的强化学习可以同时提高文档理解系统的分类性能和操作效率。

Original abstract: Real-world document classification pipelines typically apply the same sequence of models to every incoming document, regardless of its complexity or type. This leads to inefficient use of compute and human resources: simple documents are over-processed while difficult ones may not receive enough scrutiny. We introduce DocHRL, a hierarchical reinforcement learning framework that learns to adaptively and dynamically select the most cost-effective classification policy on a per-document basis. DocHRL formulates document classification as a sequential decision problem with a two-level policy hierarchy: a top-level policy selects among broad options (vision classifiers, LLMs, OCR, and human-in-the-loop review), while option-specific sub-policies choose the concrete model or tool to invoke. The reward signal is the negative total expected cost, which captures inference cost, cost of misclassification, and cost of human labelling. Trained with Proximal Policy Optimisation on the RVL-CDIP benchmark, DocHRL achieves a macro F1 of 0.973 across 16 document classes while reducing average per-document cost to 2.74 normalised units compared to substantially higher costs incurred by fixed standalone classifiers. Our results demonstrate that cost-aware reinforcement learning can simultaneously improve classification performance and operational efficiency in document understanding systems.

</details>


### [51] [STAIF: A Stage-wise Optimization for Complex Instruction Following](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22649)
*Jian Hong,Chen Cheng,Quan Liu,Yuhao Chen,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 16 pages, 6 figures

Selection reason: 指令遵循优化框架，包含RLVR，可迁移至agent训练。

TL;DR: 提出STAIF框架，通过分阶段优化软硬约束，提升LLM对复杂多约束指令的遵循能力，配合新数据集STAINSTRUCT，实现SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对齐方法（如DPO）在分布外或多约束场景下难以严格满足每个约束。

Method: 阶段式优化：第一阶段用多负样本偏好优化增强软约束敏感性；第二阶段用可验证奖励强化学习强制执行硬约束。

Result: 在代表性基准上超越强基线，并展现良好泛化能力。

Conclusion: STAIF有效解耦软硬约束优化，提升复杂指令遵循的准确性和鲁棒性。

中文摘要：遵循包含多个明确约束的复杂指令仍然是大语言模型（LLMs）的基本挑战。现有的对齐方法，如DPO，优化整体奖励信号，往往不太强调单个约束的严格满足，特别是在分布外或多约束设置下。本文提出STAIF，一种阶段式优化框架，将主观（软）约束的对齐与客观可验证（硬）约束的优化解耦。阶段1应用具有多个负样本的偏好优化以增强对软约束的敏感性，而阶段2应用具有可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）以强制执行对硬约束的严格合规。为了支持该方法，我们构建了STAINSTRUCT，一个高质量的双语（英语，中文）数据集，包含约31,000个复杂多约束指令。广泛的分析验证了STAIF的设计，并在代表性基准上展示了相对于强基线的先进性能，以及真正的泛化能力。

Original abstract: Following complex instructions with multiple explicit constraints remains a fundamental challenge for large language models (LLMs). Existing alignment methods, such as DPO, optimize holistic reward signals that often underemphasize strict satisfaction of individual constraints, particularly under out-of-distribution or multi-constraint settings. In this paper, we propose STAIF, a stage-wise optimization framework that decouples the alignment of subjective (soft) constraints from the optimization of objectively verifiable (hard) constraints. Stage 1 applies preference optimization with multiple negative samples to sharpen sensitivity to soft constraints, while Stage 2 applies Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) to enforce strict compliance with hard constraints. To support this method, we construct STAINSTRUCT, a high-quality bilingual (English, Chinese) dataset of approximately 31,000 complex multi-constraint instructions. Extensive analyses validate the design of STAIF and show state-of-the-art performance on representative benchmarks against strong baselines, as well as genuine generalization.

</details>


### [52] [ARdena: Scenario-driven control of real-time LLM agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22651)
*Luka Borozan,Domagoj Matijević*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 12 pages, 3 figures

Selection reason: 实时LLM agent控制框架，直接相关。

TL;DR: 提出分层场景驱动的LLM控制框架，通过结构化提示实现实时运行时行为控制，无需模型微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖模型微调或对齐，难以适应实时交互中不断变化的需求。

Method: 提出分层场景驱动的LLM控制框架，结合持久上下文与场景特定约束，通过结构化提示在运行时控制代理行为。

Result: 实验表明，仅凭场景定义即可产生显著不同的交互行为，同时保持稳定的实时操作，控制有效。

Conclusion: 场景驱动提示有效控制LLM代理，无需修改模型即可适应不同交互需求。

中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLM）使得对话代理的能力日益增强，但在实时交互环境中可靠地控制其行为仍然是一个重大挑战。现有方法通常依赖于模型微调或对齐程序，这些程序难以适应不断变化的交互需求。本文介绍了分层场景驱动的LLM控制，这是一种通过结构化提示实现运行时行为控制的框架。通过将持久上下文与场景特定约束相结合，该方法允许在交互过程中修改代理行为，而无需更改底层模型。该框架在ARDena中实现，这是一个实时多模态具身代理，集成了语音交互、视觉感知、工具使用和基于化身的响应生成。所提出的方法在控制有效性、响应延迟和操作稳定性方面进行了评估。结果表明，仅凭场景定义就能产生显著不同的交互行为，同时保持稳定的实时操作，凸显了场景驱动提示在控制LLM代理方面的有效性。

Original abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have enabled increasingly capable conversational agents, but reliably controlling their behavior in real-time interactive environments remains a significant challenge. Existing approaches often rely on model fine-tuning or alignment procedures that are difficult to adapt to changing interaction requirements. This paper introduces layered scenario-driven LLM control, a framework that enables runtime behavior control through structured prompting. By combining persistent context with scenario-specific constraints, the approach allows agent behavior to be modified during interaction without changing the underlying model. The framework is implemented in ARDena, a real-time multimodal embodied agent that integrates speech interaction, visual perception, tool use, and avatar-based response generation. The proposed approach is evaluated with respect to control effectiveness, response latency, and operational stability. The results demonstrate that scenario definitions alone can produce substantially different interaction behaviors while maintaining stable real-time operation, highlighting the effectiveness of scenario-driven prompting for controlling LLM agents.

</details>


### [53] [KG2Code: Bridging Knowledge Graphs and Large Language Models via Executable Code for Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22652)
*Yike Wu,Nan Hu,Guilin Qi,Guohui Xiao,Chen Jiang,Xinchun Zou,Yuchen Lu,Songlin Zhai,Yongrui Chen,Yuyang Zhang,Xiaoguang Li,Lifeng Shang,Jiaoyan Chen,Jeff Z. Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 知识图谱转代码生成，含agent组件。

TL;DR: 提出KG2Code方法，将知识图谱转换为代码表示，并基于此构建KG2Code-QA框架，将KGQA视为代码生成任务，通过自动化构建代码语料库训练LLM，在零样本KGQA中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KG增强LLM方法（RAG、Agent、SPARQL）存在结构信息丢失、推理不忠实、灵活性和泛化能力有限的问题。

Method: 提出KG2Code将KG转换为代码表示，并设计KG2Code-QA框架将KGQA转化为代码生成任务，生成可验证推理轨迹和可执行代码；同时构建自动化流水线生成大规模高质量代码语料库训练开源LLM。

Result: 在KGQA任务上显著优于现有KG增强LLM方法，并展现出对未见KG的强泛化能力。

Conclusion: KG2Code通过代码化表示和代码生成范式有效解决了现有方法的局限性，提升了KGQA性能与泛化性。

中文摘要：近年来的研究探索了知识图谱与大型语言模型的集成，以增强它们在知识密集型下游任务（尤其是知识图谱问答）上的性能。现有方法主要通过基于检索增强生成、基于智能体和基于SPARQL的方式将LLM与KG结合。尽管这些方法取得了显著成功，但仍存在结构信息丢失、推理不忠实以及灵活性和泛化能力有限等局限性。为了解决这些问题，本文提出KG2Code，一种新颖的方法，将知识图谱转换为基于代码的表示，保留结构语义，同时自然对齐现代LLM的代码感知预训练。基于KG2Code，进一步引入KG2Code-QA作为KGQA框架，将KGQA表述为代码生成任务。这种表述能够生成可验证的推理轨迹和可执行代码，从而显著减轻幻觉的影响。此外，开发了一个自动化流水线，用于构建大规模、高质量的代码语料库，以有效训练开源LLM在KG2Code-QA上的能力。训练后，LLM能够在零样本场景下执行KGQA。大量实验表明，所提出的方法在KGQA上显著优于现有的KG增强LLM方法，同时对未见过的KG表现出强大的泛化能力。代码和数据可在GitHub上获取。

Original abstract: Recent research has explored the integration of knowledge graphs (KGs) with large language models (LLMs) to enhance their performance on downstream knowledge-intensive tasks, particularly knowledge graph question answering (KGQA). Existing approaches primarily combine LLMs with KGs through retrieval-augmented generation (RAG)-based, agent-based, and SPARQL-based methods. Although these methods have achieved notable success, they still suffer from several limitations, including structural information loss, unfaithful reasoning, and limited flexibility and generalization. To address these challenges, this paper proposes KG2Code, a novel approach that transforms knowledge graphs into a code-based representation, preserving structural semantics while naturally aligning with the code-aware pretraining of modern LLMs. Based on KG2Code, KG2Code-QA is further introduced as a KGQA framework that formulates KGQA as a code generation task. This formulation enables the generation of verifiable reasoning traces and executable code, thereby substantially mitigating the impact of hallucinations. In addition, an automated pipeline is developed to construct a large-scale, high-quality code corpus for effectively training open-source LLMs on KG2Code-QA. After training, LLMs are able to perform KGQA in zero-shot scenarios. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed approach significantly outperforms existing KG-enhanced LLM methods for KGQA, while exhibiting strong generalization to unseen KGs. The code and data are available at Github.

</details>


### [54] [Do Language Models Converge to Themselves? Recursive Self-Refinement as Textual Relaxation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22653)
*Xuening Wu,Qianya Xu,Yanlan Kang,Zeping Chen,Yubin Liu,Shenqin Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 递归自精炼动力学研究，对agent自我改进有启发。

TL;DR: 研究了LLM递归自优化的长期动态，发现快速收敛到软不动点，并提出基于编辑幅度饱和的停止标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对LLM递归细化工作流程的长期动态理解不足，不清楚重复修订是否会无限优化还是收敛到稳定形式。

Method: 使用GPT-5.5对50篇ICML 2025和15篇ICML 2020摘要进行10步细化轨迹，分析归一化编辑距离、不动点、单词计数稳定性、指数松弛和LLM评判评估。

Result: 细化轨迹迅速饱和，大多数编辑在早期迭代完成，然后进入软不动点；确定性解码更快达到精确不动点；平均编辑幅度呈指数衰减；外部评估显示收敛摘要质量提升。

Conclusion: 支持LLM自优化的动力系统视角，并建议基于编辑幅度饱和的实用停止标准。

中文摘要：大型语言模型越来越多地用于递归细化工作流程，其中初始草稿由同一模型反复修订。尽管其使用日益广泛，但此类工作流程的长期动态仍然知之甚少。重复细化是否会无限期地持续改进输出，还是会收敛到一个稳定的文本形式？我们将递归自优化作为一种动力学过程进行研究，其中重复的LLM修订将文本驱动到模型偏好的软不动点区域。使用GPT-5.5，我们在默认温度和确定性解码下，为50篇ICML 2025摘要生成了10步细化轨迹，并额外评估了15篇ICML 2020摘要。我们分析了归一化编辑距离、精确和近似不动点、单词计数稳定性、指数松弛以及外部LLM作为评判者的评估。在所有设置中，细化轨迹迅速饱和。大多数编辑发生在最初几次迭代中，之后轨迹进入一个仅有微小表面变化的软不动点区域。确定性解码比默认温度解码更早达到精确不动点，并且表现出更小的剩余波动，而两者都实现了通用的近似收敛。平均编辑幅度遵循一致的指数松弛模式，这表明收敛到模型偏好的文本均衡，而非开放式优化。外部评估表明，收敛后的摘要提高了清晰度、简洁性和科学风格，同时保留了技术含义。这些发现支持了LLM自优化的动力系统观点，并基于编辑幅度饱和提出了实用的停止标准。

Original abstract: Large language models are increasingly used in recursive refinement workflows, where an initial draft is repeatedly revised by the same model. Despite their growing use, the long-term dynamics of such workflows remain poorly understood. Does repeated refinement continue to improve outputs indefinitely, or does it converge toward a stable textual form?
  We study recursive self-refinement as a dynamical process in which repeated LLM revision drives text toward a model-preferred soft fixed-point region. Using GPT-5.5, we generate 10-step refinement trajectories for 50 ICML 2025 abstracts under both default-temperature and deterministic decoding, and additionally evaluate 15 ICML 2020 abstracts. We analyze normalized edit distance, exact and approximate fixed points, word-count stability, exponential relaxation, and external LLM-as-a-judge evaluation.
  Across all settings, refinement trajectories rapidly saturate. Most edits occur within the first few iterations, after which trajectories enter a soft fixed-point region with only minor surface-level changes. Deterministic decoding reaches exact fixed points earlier and exhibits smaller residual fluctuations than default-temperature decoding, while both achieve universal approximate convergence. The average edit magnitude follows a consistent exponential relaxation pattern, suggesting convergence toward a model-preferred textual equilibrium rather than open-ended optimization. External evaluation indicates that converged abstracts improve clarity, conciseness, and scientific style while preserving technical meaning.
  These findings support a dynamical-systems view of LLM self-refinement and motivate practical stopping criteria based on edit-magnitude saturation.

</details>


### [55] [EventOD: Event-Aware OD Flow Generation via LLM-Guided Semantic Modulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22655)
*Jie Zhao,Jie Feng,Can Rong,Zhihan Hou,Peng Lu,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

Selection reason: 提出LLM引导的语义调制框架，包含控制向量推断、轻量化适配模块和检索增强，可迁移至agent设计的工具使用与适应机制。

TL;DR: EventOD利用大型语言模型推断事件语义，通过轻量级模块自适应调整预训练OD生成器，在破坏性事件下提升OD流估计的准确性和分布保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度OD模型在极端事件下性能退化，且为每个事件重新训练生成器不现实，需要一种事件自适应的OD生成方法。

Method: EventOD框架：先通过LLM从事件观测中推断区域控制向量，再用AlphaNet和BetaNet校准语义变化强度，结合检索增强回退路径，将事件特征注入预训练图扩散OD模型，实现输入级调制自适应。

Result: 在美国飓风和疫情导致的县域流动性实验中，EventOD在重建准确性和分布保真度上持续优于强基线。

Conclusion: EventOD通过结构化事件语义引导预训练模型，无需更新参数即可实现事件感知的OD流量估计，有效应对破坏性事件。

中文摘要：在破坏性事件下估算起点-终点（OD）流量对于灾害响应和城市韧性至关重要。现有的深度OD模型在常规流动性数据上训练，当极端事件突然改变区域功能和人口活动时，性能往往会下降，而在有限的事件时间监督下为每个事件重新训练新生成器是不切实际的。我们提出EventOD，一种事件自适应OD生成框架，利用结构化事件语义引导预训练的OD生成器。EventOD首先使用大型语言模型从粗略的事件观测中推断区域级别的功能和人口统计控制向量。然后学习两个轻量级自适应模块AlphaNet和BetaNet，以校准这些语义变化的幅度，并进一步引入检索增强的回退路径以应对稀疏监督的场景。生成的事件条件特征通过输入级调制注入到预训练的图扩散OD模型中，实现事件感知自适应而无需更新生成器参数。在美国各县的飓风和疫情导致的流动性实验中，EventOD在重建准确性和分布保真度方面均持续优于强基线。源代码可在https://anonymous.4open.science/r/EventOD-5C11/获取。

Original abstract: Estimating origin-destination (OD) flows under disruptive events is important for disaster response and urban resilience. Existing deep OD models trained on routine mobility often degrade when extreme events abruptly alter regional functions and population activities, while retraining a new generator for each event is impractical under limited event-time supervision. We propose EventOD, an event-adaptive OD generation framework that steers a pretrained OD generator using structured event semantics. EventOD first uses a large language model to infer region-level functional and demographic control vectors from coarse event observations. It then learns two lightweight adaptation modules, AlphaNet and BetaNet, to calibrate the magnitude of these semantic shifts, and further introduces a retrieval-augmented fallback pathway for scenarios with sparse supervision. The resulting event-conditioned features are injected into a pretrained graph diffusion OD model through input-level modulation, enabling event-aware adaptation without updating generator parameters. Experiments on hurricane- and pandemic-induced mobility across U.S. counties show that EventOD consistently improves both reconstruction accuracy and distributional fidelity over strong baselines. Source code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/EventOD-5C11/.

</details>


### [56] [Reinforcement Learning for Heterogeneous Sensor Selection in Maritime Surveillance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22667)
*Andrei Starodubov,Yaqub Aris Prabowo,Andreas Hadjipieris,Roberto Galeazzi,Ioannis Kyriakides*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 5 pages, 4 figures, submitted to the IEEE MetroSea 2026 Conference: Special Session 13: Object Detection, Tracking, and Sensor Fusion for Maritime Situational Awareness

Selection reason: 用RL策略进行传感器选择，可类比agent的工具选择与决策，但应用领域为海事监控，通用性有限。

TL;DR: 提出信息增益引导的强化学习传感器选择框架，用于船只跟踪，只激活一个传感器达到接近全传感器跟踪性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 避免始终激活所有传感器或进行昂贵的在线期望信息增益计算，通过学习策略高效选择单个传感器。

Method: 使用PPO代理，基于贝叶斯序贯蒙特卡罗跟踪器的置信状态和多种特征，以可观测性门控的实现信息增益为奖励。

Result: 单传感器策略跟踪性能接近全传感器，且避免了在线熵搜索的计算开销。

Conclusion: 强化学习可有效用于传感器调度，平衡跟踪精度与计算效率。

中文摘要：本文提出了一种信息增益引导的强化学习传感器选择框架，用于异构海上传感器网络中单船跟踪。该方法的动机源于信息论传感器管理：不是激活所有传感器或重复执行计算昂贵的在线期望信息增益评估，而是学习策略在每个决策时刻选择一个与跟踪相关的传感器。贝叶斯序贯蒙特卡罗跟踪器根据含噪声的测量值估计船舶状态，并为非线性和非高斯条件下的调度提供置信度表示。近端策略优化代理选择部署在塞浦路斯Ayia Napa Marina的CMMI智能码头试验台地理参考仿真中的五个传感器之一。代理观察置信状态、检测历史、覆盖范围、传感器几何和实现信息增益特征。奖励定义为由可观测性掩码门控的实现信息增益项。最终测试仿真将提出的框架与随机单传感器选择、同时使用所有传感器的始终在线感知以及我们之前工作中提出的期望信息增益传感器选择基线进行比较。结果表明，学习策略在激活一个传感器每次决策时间步且避免期望信息增益选择所需的计算昂贵的在线熵搜索的同时，实现了接近始终在线感知的跟踪性能。

Original abstract: This paper presents an information-gain-guided reinforcement-learning sensor-selection framework for single-vessel tracking in heterogeneous maritime sensor networks. The proposed approach is motivated by information-theoretic sensor management: instead of activating all sensors or repeatedly performing computationally expensive online expected-information-gain evaluation, a learned policy selects one tracking-relevant sensor at each decision epoch. A Bayesian sequential Monte Carlo tracker estimates the vessel state from noisy measurements and provides a belief representation for scheduling under nonlinear and non-Gaussian conditions. A Proximal Policy Optimization agent selects one of five sensors deployed in a georeferenced simulation of the CMMI Smart Marina testbed at Ayia Napa Marina, Cyprus. The agent observes belief-state, detection-history, coverage, sensor-geometry, and realized-information-gain features. The reward is defined as a realized-information-gain term gated by an observability mask. Final-test simulations compare the proposed framework with random single-sensor selection, always-on sensing using all sensors simultaneously, and the expected-information-gain sensor-selection baseline proposed in our previous work. Results show that the learned policy achieves tracking performance close to always-on sensing while activating only one sensor per decision time step and avoiding the computationally expensive online entropy search required by expected-information-gain selection.

</details>


### [57] [AIR-BENCH Live: An Evolving Safety Benchmark for Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22671)
*Rohan Naphade,Minzhou Pan,Bo Li*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 11 pages, 7 figures, 3 tables

Selection reason: 安全基准包含多agent提示生成与更新，对agent安全评估有启发，但非核心方法贡献。

TL;DR: AIR-BENCH Live是一个自我演化的基础模型安全基准，通过自动化更新管道吸收新法规并生成新提示，扩展了风险分类并评估了多个模型的安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全基准无法跟上模型改进和法规变化，需要动态演化的基准来持续评估AI风险。

Method: 使用自动化管道监控政府法规并更新风险分类，结合多智能体角色驱动算法生成多语言提示，并不断迭代。

Result: 基准从314个风险扩展到335个，新提示比旧版难0.06分，模型安全性差异大且非英语提示安全性更低。

Conclusion: AIR-BENCH Live通过持续更新保持与快速发展的AI领域同步，是评估前沿模型安全性的有效工具。

中文摘要：基础模型安全基准测试捕捉了其发布时的人工智能风险：随着模型改进和政府通过新的人工智能安全立法，其风险分类变得不全面，其攻击提示变得无效。我们提出了AIR-BENCH Live，这是AIR-BENCH 2024的一个自我演化的后继版本。一个自动化更新管道监控政府法规，并将新政策与当前的四个层级风险分类进行匹配，要么将它们归入现有类别，要么提出新的细粒度类别。然后，一个多智能体、角色驱动的提示生成算法生成逼真的多语言提示，所需的人工审核最少，并为使用现代越狱技术的改进留出空间。该算法用于彻底改革遗留提示并为新类别生成提示。在我们当前的版本中，该管道已将基准从314个细粒度风险扩展到335个，其中21个新类别来自七个司法管辖区的31个真正新颖的政策条款。评估14个近期模型，我们发现安全性差异很大（在根据自身行为评判的模型中，从0.17到0.89），现代化后的提示平均比2024年版本难0.06分，最大的下降集中在最合规的模型上，并且大多数模型在非英语提示上的安全性略低。通过不断吸收新法规并重新生成提示，AIR-BENCH Live旨在与快速发展的领域同步演变。

Original abstract: Foundation-model safety benchmarks capture the AI risks of their time of publication: as models improve and governments pass new AI-safety legislation, their risk taxonomies become incomprehensive and their attack prompts become ineffective. We present AIR-BENCH Live, a self-evolving successor to AIR-BENCH 2024. An automated update pipeline monitors government regulation and classifies new policies against the current four-tier risk taxonomy, either matching them to existing categories or proposing new granular categories. Then, a multi-agent, persona-driven prompt generation algorithm generates realistic, multilingual prompts with minimal human review, leaving room for improvement with modern jail breaking techniques. This algorithm is used to overhaul legacy prompts and generate prompts for new categories. In our current version, the pipeline has expanded the benchmark from 314 to 335 granular risks, with the 21 new categories drawing from 31 truly novel policy clauses across seven jurisdictions. Evaluating 14 recent models, we find a wide safety spread (from 0.17 to 0.89 among the models judged on their own behavior), that the modernized prompts are on average 0.06 points harder than the 2024 set, with the largest drops concentrated among the most compliant models, and that most models are modestly less safe on non-English prompts. By continuously absorbing new regulation and regenerating prompts, AIR-BENCH Live is designed to evolve alongside a fast-moving field.

</details>


### [58] [A Vocabulary for Multi-Agent Automated Research Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22682)
*Bardiya Akhbari*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 22 pages, 4 figures, 3 tables

Selection reason: 提出多agent自动研究系统的设计词汇，直接贡献于agent架构描述与比较，可迁移性强。

TL;DR: 提出了一个用于描述和比较自动化研究系统设计选择的词汇表，涵盖代理、操作、通信、信息可见性、动作选择、运行初始化和输出评估等八个方面，并通过实例展示了其适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了更易于描述和比较自动化研究系统的设计选择，并将结构性问题转化为可检验的假设，同时将关于系统“缺乏品味”的模糊抱怨分解为可解决的生成性和评估性品味问题。

Method: 通过定义一个包含八个方面的词汇表来描述自动化研究系统的设计，包括代理、操作、调用权限、通信方式、信息可见性、动作选择、运行初始化和输出评估，并利用轨迹分布来刻画随机性。

Result: 词汇表成功覆盖了结构差异大的多种自动研究系统，并将设计问题转化为可检验的选择，同时区分了生成性品味和评估性品味。

Conclusion: 该词汇表为自动化研究系统的设计和比较提供了一个系统的框架，有助于澄清设计选择并改进系统评估。

中文摘要：我们引入了一个用于自动化研究系统的词汇表，这些系统由一个或多个代理构建，旨在使其设计选择更易于描述和比较。该词汇表指定了：1) 代理是谁，2) 系统中可用的操作有哪些，3) 谁可以调用它们，4) 代理如何通信，5) 在单次运行和多次运行之间哪些信息可见，6) 如何选择下一个动作，7) 运行如何开始，以及8) 输出如何评估。一条轨迹记录从输入任务到返回产物的单次运行。由于代理、操作和初始化可能是随机的，对同一任务的重复运行会诱导出轨迹分布而非单一行为。我们的词汇表将结构性问题（如代理何时应该通信、获得或失去某种能力，或在运行间传递信息）转化为可检验的选择。它还将评估器作为系统的一个组件，因为报告的性能提升取决于代理分数与真实质量的匹配程度。这种分离还将“这些系统缺乏品味”这一模糊抱怨分解为两个具有不同解决方案的失败。生成性品味是系统在观察到任何分数之前提出新颖轨迹的速率，而评估性品味是代理分数与其应匹配质量之间的差距。我们在最近的自动研究系统上实例化该词汇表，以说明它涵盖了结构上差异很大的设计。

Original abstract: We introduce a vocabulary for automated research systems built from one or more agents to make their design choices easier to describe and compare. The vocabulary specifies 1) who the agents are, 2) what operations are available in the system, 3) who may invoke them, 4) how agents communicate, 5) what information is visible within and across runs, 6) how the next action is chosen, 7) how a run begins, and 8) how outputs are evaluated. A trajectory records one run from the input task to the returned artifact. Because agents, operations, and initialization may be stochastic, repeated runs on the same task induce a distribution over trajectories rather than a single behavior.
  Our vocabulary turns structural design questions, such as when agents should communicate, gain or lose a capability, or carry information across runs, into testable choices. It also makes the evaluator a component of the system, since reported gains depend on how closely the proxy score matches true quality.
